Result: Mistral重写后动词多样性从0.77升至0.92，检测器更易识别；检测分数追踪统计复杂度，导致正式人类写作76.3%误报率。事件潜在空间检测在改写下事件序列Jaccard仅0.067（87%改变），同形异义词导致70%动词改变（Jaccard=0.30），最佳领域AUC仅0.577。RoBERTa鲁棒性局限于其使用的特征，结构抽象未提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 当前监督式检测器主要利用统计复杂度而非语义特征，极易对高复杂度人类文本误报；事件基潜在空间检测对扰动高度敏感，结构抽象未能提供鲁棒性增益。未来检测器设计需超越表层统计特征，探索更本质的语义不变量。

Abstract: Supervised AI-text detectors report high benchmark accuracy, but it is not clear what their decisions are based on. We analyze a RoBERTa-based detector under semantic, structural, and tokenizer-level perturbations, using the M4 dataset (N = 10,000) and controlled generations (N = 300). When Mistral-7B-Instruct was asked to make machine text sound more human, Verb Diversity rose from 0.77 to 0.92 and the outputs became easier to detect. Detection scores appear to track statistical complexity, which also leads to a 76.3% false-positive rate on formal human writing. As a control, we evaluate event-based Latent Space detection. Paraphrasing changed 87% of its event sequences (Jaccard = 0.067), and homoglyphs altered 70% of the extracted verbs even though extraction still ran (Jaccard = 0.30). Its best domain AUC was 0.577. RoBERTa's robustness seems specific to the features it uses, and structural abstraction did not make detection more robust.

</details>


### [44] [Building Interpretable Feature Representations for Resume-Vacancy Matching by Distilling Production LLM Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03112)
*Ilya Chekin,Vyacheslav Malyugin,Vladimir Chirkov,Mikhail Yurushkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种两阶段方法，用于招聘中候选人与职位的匹配，提供可解释的匹配维度而非单一不透明分数。第一阶段使用基于LLM的标注器离线生成标签，第二阶段将其蒸馏为轻量级双编码器在线服务，实现CPU上高效部署。在生产反馈数据上，模型与招聘人员决策一致率达95.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘匹配需要可解释的证据，而非仅提供不透明的相关性分数，招聘人员需要看到候选人匹配的具体维度以便决策。

Method: 两部分方法：(1) 基于LLM的标注器，通过招聘人员反馈迭代优化提示词和特征定义，仅用于离线标注；(2) 从标注器蒸馏的特征双编码器，采用LoRA适配的嵌入骨干网络加紧凑的逐维度头，运行于CPU，服务所有在线请求。两部分持续改进：提示词随反馈修订，双编码器在更新标签上重新训练。训练数据为168,772个标注的职位-简历对。

Result: 在927个招聘人员记录的生产反馈样本上，部署的学生模型与记录决策一致888次，一致率95.79%。这是操作性的、非盲测的一致性，而非独立人工评估。

Conclusion: 该方法成功部署于生产环境，通过LLM标注器与蒸馏双编码器的协作，实现了可解释、高效且持续改进的招聘匹配系统，招聘人员反馈确认了其高实用性。

Abstract: Matching candidates to vacancies is central to recruitment, and a recruiter needs to see why a candidate fits, not only a single opaque relevance score. We provide this evidence as named, interpretable matching dimensions recruiters can act on - eight in our current deployment. We propose a two-part approach. The first is an LLM-based labeler whose prompts and feature definitions were refined from recruiter feedback while it served as an earlier production matching stage. In the current architecture, it is used only for offline labeling and is not called on online requests. The second is a feature bi-encoder distilled from it: a LoRA-adapted embedding backbone with compact per-dimension heads that runs on CPU and serves all online requests. Both parts keep improving: prompts are revised as feedback arrives, and the bi-encoder is retrained on the updated labels. The model is trained on 168,772 labeled vacancy-resume pairs (17,921 vacancies and 180,030 resumes). Recruiters using the service can confirm or revise surfaced feature predictions. On 927 recruiter-recorded values from this selected production-feedback subset, the deployed student agrees with the recorded decisions in 888 cases (95.79%). This is operational, non-blinded agreement rather than an independent human evaluation.

</details>


### [45] [Investigating the Role of Reasoning-Language Alignment in Monolingual Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03136)
*Oliver Hauck,Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究检索增强生成（RAG）中推理语言对齐的影响。构建德语单语RAG问答测试平台（基于《黑暗之眼》TRPG设定），发现将推理语言与查询及检索文档语言对齐（德语）优于强制法语推理，即使模型法语基准更高，证明收益源于语言对齐而非语言能力。优势随检索上下文丰富度和结构感知增强，但强制德语推理仅能达到模型原生英语推理水平，无法超越，表明需要原生多语言推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，强制大语言模型（LLM）用非英语推理会降低准确率，但仅在短提示词设定下验证。论文旨在探究这一结论是否适用于RAG场景——模型需阅读并整合大量目标语言的检索证据。

Method: 构建完全单语德语RAG问答测试平台，领域选取德语资料丰富但模型无法凭记忆回答的桌面角色扮演游戏《黑暗之眼》设定。在agentic RAG系统中变更强制推理语言（德语、法语、英语），对比推理语言与查询/检索文档语言对齐与否的效果，并测试不同检索上下文丰富度和结构感知条件下的表现。

Result: 1. 推理语言与查询及检索文档语言对齐（德语）显著优于不对齐（法语），尽管模型法语基准分数更高，收益源于对齐而非语言熟练度。2. 当检索上下文更丰富且具结构感知时，对齐优势扩大。3. 强制德语推理仅能达到模型原生无约束英语推理水平，无法超越，揭示原生多语言推理的必要性。

Conclusion: 在RAG任务中，推理语言与检索语料语言对齐能提升性能，且随证据密度增加而增强；但当前模型仍无法通过强制推理超越原生英语推理上限，未来需发展真正的原生多语言推理能力。作者公开了测试平台与QA基准以供后续研究。

Abstract: Reasoning traces improve large language models (LLMs), but current models are trained to reason mostly in English. It has been shown that forcing a model to reason in another language degrades accuracy, even when the reasoning language matches the language of the prompt -- but only for a setting where the model reasons over a short prompt. Here, we ask whether the same holds for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where the model must read and integrate a large amount of retrieved evidence in the target language. To study this, we build a fully monolingual German RAG question-answering testbed over the fictional world of the tabletop role-playing game The Dark Eye, a domain that is richly documented in German but too niche for the model to answer from memory, so that it has to rely on retrieval. Varying the forced reasoning language of an agentic RAG system on this testbed, we find that aligning the reasoning language with the language of the query and the retrieved documents helps. Forced German reasoning outperforms forced French, although the model benchmarks higher in French, so the benefit comes from alignment and not from language proficiency. The advantage grows when the retrieved context is richer and structure-aware. However, forced German only reaches the level of the model's native, unconstrained English reasoning without surpassing it, showing that native multilingual reasoning is needed. We publicly release the testbed and QA benchmark.

</details>


### [46] [Predicting Steering Vectors and Adapter Weights for Few-Shot Author-Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03163)
*Leonard Popp,Danni Liu,Supriti Sinhamahapatra,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从少量样本将大语言模型适配到个别作者的科学写作风格，提出三种风格条件生成方法，并发现风格模仿与生成质量存在权衡：超网络预测LoRA适配器在已见和未见作者上均达到最佳平衡；同时证明手工提取与预测的引导向量近似正交却性能相当，说明风格条件化可容纳多个无关方向而非单一最优轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学写作因正式惯例导致表面变化极小，且作者主题与风格易纠缠，使得从少量样本学习个体风格极具挑战。现有基于预定义风格清单的引导方法无法捕捉个体细微差异，且需固定主题以分离风格信号。

Method: 提出三种风格条件摘要生成方法：(1) 对比激活引导，将作者摘要与同内容的风格中性生成对比以提取引导向量；(2) 预测引导向量的网络，从少量样本直接回归引导向量；(3) 预测LoRA适配器的超网络，生成针对特定作者的低秩适配参数。均在作者层面操作，固定主题对比。

Result: 实验显示微调虽获取最多风格信号但牺牲流畅度；超网络在风格模仿与输出质量间实现最佳权衡，且对未见作者泛化良好。对比激活引导无需预定义风格清单即优于基于清单的方法。分析发现手工与预测引导向量近似正交但得分相当，表明风格条件化至少存在两个无关的有效方向。

Conclusion: 风格条件化不依赖单一最优轴，可容纳多个正交方向；超网络预测LoRA适配器是少样本作者风格适配的有效方案，能同时保持风格保真度与生成质量，且泛化至未见作者。

Abstract: Adapting large language models to an individual author's style from a few examples is challenging, and scientific writing sharpens the difficulty: formal conventions leave little surface variation, and authors write about their own topics, so extracted ``style'' easily entangles with content. We study style-conditioned abstract generation from a few example abstracts per author and propose three methods: (1) contrastive activation steering, (2) a network that predicts steering vectors, and (3) a hypernetwork that predicts LoRA adapters. We find a consistent trade-off between style imitation and output quality: fine-tuning buys most of the available style signal but forfeits fluency, while the hypernetwork achieves the best trade-off on both seen and unseen authors. Our steering operates at author level, contrasting an author's abstracts against style-neutral generations for the same content. This holds topic fixed, removes the need for a predefined style inventory, and outperforms inventory-based steering. % [EDIT 1a] softened "no single optimal axis" claim Moreover, our analyses demonstrate that manually extracted and predicted steering vectors are near-orthogonal yet score comparably, indicating that style conditioning here can admit at least two unrelated directions rather than requiring one particular axis.

</details>


### [47] [Hindsight-Guided Rationale Distillation for Rare Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03176)
*Aarav Singh,Animesh Pathak,Navyansh Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在 ZebraMap 数据集上利用事后指导蒸馏进行罕见病诊断，发现未过滤的学生模型因复制教师推理中的“GT 幻觉”导致性能未显著提升，而过滤后的学生模型通过正则移除污染槽位实现了小但显著的准确率提升，揭示了频率依赖的知识迁移和校准差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索通过事后指导蒸馏将大模型（8B 教师）的推理能力迁移到小模型（1.5B 学生）以提升罕见病诊断准确率，同时识别并解决蒸馏过程中引入的标签污染问题。

Method: 使用 8B 教师模型在生成时观察真实诊断标签产生思维链轨迹，对 1.5B 学生模型进行监督微调；引入正则过滤器移除推理中嵌入的 “ground truth is X” 短语，对比过滤前后学生模型与教师模型的性能差异，并进行统计显著性检验。

Result: 绝对准确率均较低。过滤后的学生模型较教师实现小但显著的准确率提升（p<0.001），主要集中在表现较好的疾病；未过滤学生模型无显著优势（p=0.129）。未过滤学生在 33.9% 推理中复现 GT 幻觉，导致错误标签时严重性能下降；正则过滤将污染降至近零。量化了增益-成本权衡，发现频率依赖的知识迁移及 SFT 未能弥合的校准差距。

Conclusion: 事后蒸馏本身不足以提升性能，关键在于移除教师推理中嵌入的真实标签短语（GT 幻觉）造成的污染。过滤后的蒸馏虽带来统计显著增益，但幅度有限；未来工作需解决频率依赖的知识迁移缺失和校准差距问题。

Abstract: We study hindsight-guided distillation for rare disease diagnosis on ZebraMap: a 1.5B student is fine-tuned on chain-of-thought traces from a 8B teacher that observes the ground-truth diagnosis during generation. Absolute accuracy remains low for all models - the task is hard at this scale - but within this ceiling a filtered variant (StudentF) achieves a small, statistically significant accuracy advantage over the teacher (p < 0.001), concentrated in better-represented diseases. The unfiltered student does not significantly outperform the teacher (p = 0.129), establishing that contamination filtering - not hindsight distillation alone - drives the gain. The gap traces to an artifact we term GT hallucination. Label-visible generation causes the teacher to embed "ground truth is X" phrases in its reasoning chain; SFT copies the pattern. At inference, the unfiltered student reproduces the phrase in 33.9% of cases, with severe accuracy degradation when the hallucinated label is wrong. A regex filter removing these slots reduces contamination to near-zero, producing the observed gain - though the effect remains small. We precisely quantify this gain-cost tradeoff, document frequency-dependent knowledge transfer absent from the RL-trained teacher, and characterize a calibration gap that SFT does not close - identifying both as directions for future work.

</details>


### [48] [Not Until the Evidence Says So: Teaching LLM Investigators When to Close a Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03190)
*Tingzhu Bi,Ping Wang,Meng Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在事故/缺陷/故障调查中判断证据是否足以结案的能力。发现未训练模型严重高估证据（97%），前沿模型虽能识别正确原因（84%）但仍高估证据（91%）且错误结案。作者构建了Nautil数据集（731个经审计案例），提出三维评估框架，通过监督微调+强化学习显著提升模型依据证据结案的能力：微调使过度自称从97%降至35%，正确非过度结论从3%升至43%；RL使平衡准确率从69.2提升至83.3，但以证据依赖性下降为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事故调查需要做出一个普通问答不面临的判断：现有证据是否足以结案。现有LLM在这类任务中普遍存在过度自称证据、错误结案的问题，且由于案例来源强相关标签，评估该能力非同寻常。需要系统研究LLM的结案判断能力，并开发相应的评估方法和改进技术。

Method: 1. 构建Nautil数据集：731个来自航空、铁路、海事、化工安全、车辆缺陷和生产服务器事故的经审计案例，含教师轨迹、分布外测试集和反事实证据版本。2. 提出三维评估框架：结案准确率（对比仅读来源的规则基线及各来源内）、证据依赖性（移除结论依据检查模型是否停止结案）、结论与缺口质量（人工清单评估）。3. 两阶段训练：先在教师轨迹上微调9B模型，再用仅奖励结案决策的强化学习优化。

Result: 微调后：移除依据使结案率比对照组下降26个百分点；过度自称从97%降至35%；正确非过度结论从3%升至43%。RL后：平衡准确率从69.2提升至83.3（与教师持平），来源内准确率从60.4提升至74.1，但证据依赖性有所下降。

Conclusion: 通过结构化轨迹微调和针对结案决策的强化学习，可以显著提升LLM在事故调查中依据证据进行结案判断的能力，使其更忠实于证据、减少过度自称。但RL优化决策准确性会以牺牲证据依赖性为代价，揭示了准确性与忠实性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Accident, defect and outage investigations end with a decision that ordinary question answering never faces: whether the evidence gathered so far is enough to close the case. We study this decision for LLM investigators, which request evidence from a case file, revise their hypotheses, and either close the case with a conclusion grounded in what they read or leave it open and name what is missing. This judgment does not come with capability: an untrained 9B model overstates its evidence in 97% of its answers, and a frontier model that identifies the right cause in 84% of cases still overstates in 91% and closes 17 of the 41 cases whose official finding is "cause undetermined". Measuring it is also non-trivial: the source of a case largely predicts its label, and a rule that reads only the source reaches 83.0 balanced accuracy on our test cases. We therefore evaluate closure with three tests: closure accuracy, reported against this rule and within each source; evidence dependence, which removes the grounds of a conclusion and checks whether the model stops closing; and conclusion and gap quality, a judged checklist of what the model asserts and what it says is missing. We build Nautil, 731 audited cases from aviation, rail, maritime, chemical-safety and vehicle-defect reports and production server incidents, with teacher trajectories, an out-of-distribution test set and counterfactual evidence versions. Fine-tuning a 9B model on these trajectories makes its closures follow the evidence: removing the grounds lowers its closure rate by 26 points relative to a matched control, overstatement falls from 97% to 35%, and correct, non-overstated conclusions rise from 3% to 43%. Reinforcement learning that rewards only the closure decision then raises balanced accuracy from 69.2 to 83.3, on par with the teacher, and within-source accuracy from 60.4 to 74.1, at some cost in evidence dependence.

</details>


### [49] [Source Preference in the Wild: How LLM Agents Favor Items by Source, and How to Reduce It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03195)
*Jonghyun Song,Haewon Park,Jeonghoon Shim,Woojung Song,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统性地调查了LLM代理在端到端搜索任务中的来源偏好现象，发现12个代理模型在三个领域中一致偏好某些来源而排斥其他来源，这种偏好甚至能覆盖项目对需求的满足程度。研究揭示了来源标识本身影响选择的机制，并提出了训练捷径和先入为主两个形成偏好的路径，同时验证了补充缺失信息和反偏见提示词可以有效缓解该偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理越来越多地代表用户做出购买、预订、引用等决策，其对特定来源（网站或服务）的偏好将直接塑造用户接收的信息和被选中的来源。理解这种来源偏好的存在、强度、机制及缓解方法，对于保证代理决策的公平性、准确性和用户利益至关重要。

Method: 在三个领域（产品购买、酒店预订、论文引用）中，对比满足相同需求且位置相同的不同来源项目，测试12个LLM代理模型的端到端搜索选择行为。通过隐藏来源标识、重新标记来源、补充缺失信息、使用反先入为主提示词等干预实验，探究来源偏好的成因及缓解策略。

Result: 所有模型在每个领域都表现出显著的来源偏好，且高度一致。来源偏好能覆盖需求满足度：来自偏好来源的项目即使少满足一个需求，也有约2/3概率被选中；反向情况几乎从不发生。隐藏来源标识会削弱偏好，将项目重新标记为偏好来源能提高其被选概率。偏好形成有两条路径：训练中奖励更好项目使来源成为需求满足的捷径；信息缺失触发对来源的先入为主。补充缺失信息或使用反偏见提示词可有效减少偏好。

Conclusion: LLM代理存在普遍且强烈的来源偏好，这可能导致次优决策并损害用户利益。偏好源于训练过程中的捷径学习和推理时的先入为主。通过信息完善和提示词干预可以显著缓解该问题，为构建更公平、可靠的代理系统提供了实用方向。

Abstract: As LLM agents decide on users' behalf which product to buy, which hotel to book, or which paper to cite, a preference for items from certain sources (the sites or services they come from) shapes what users receive and which sources are selected. We study source preference in end-to-end search with 12 agent models across three domains. Comparing items from different sources that satisfy the same requirements at the same position, we find that each model prefers some sources and avoids others in every domain, largely agreeing on which. This preference can outweigh how well items satisfy the request: an item satisfying one requirement fewer is selected about two-thirds of the time when it comes from a preferred source and the better one from a dispreferred source, but almost never in the reverse case. The information identifying an item's source affects selection by itself: hiding it weakens the preference, and relabeling an item with a preferred source raises its selection rate. We test two routes to this preference: training that rewards better items can make a source a shortcut for requirement satisfaction, and missing information can trigger preconceptions about the source. Supplying missing information or a prompt countering these preconceptions reduces source preference.

</details>


### [50] [StanceEval 2026: The Second Stance Detection Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03215)
*Rasha Albalawi,Nuha Albadi,Hamzah Luqman,Asma Yamani,Maram Kurdi,Saad Ezzini,Ahmed Ashraf,Maged Al-Shaibani,Nora Alturayeif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StanceEval 2026 评估阿拉伯语立场检测的跨目标泛化，设两赛道分别测试主题相关迁移（Track 1）与完全未见目标迁移（Track 2）。顶尖系统 F_avg2 得分达 0.8994 和 0.9400，大幅超越基线。反直觉地，未见目标表现优于相关目标，可能源于极端目标极化、类别不平衡及方言/讽刺细微差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动阿拉伯语社交媒体立场检测的跨目标泛化研究，通过共享任务形式系统评估模型在主题相关迁移与跨域完全未见目标迁移两种场景下的表现，填补阿拉伯语资源匮乏、方言多样、讽刺普遍等挑战下的基准空白。

Method: 举办共享任务，吸引 12 国 80 支队伍注册，30 支提交系统，经验证后 Track 1 21 支、Track 2 13 支正式排名。参赛方法涵盖微调预训练语言模型、基于提示与检索增强的大语言模型、微调大语言模型及混合级联等多元策略。

Result: Track 1 与 Track 2 冠军系统 F_avg2 分别达 0.8994 与 0.9400，显著高于最强基线（0.7366 与 0.7475）。意外发现：未见目标（Track 2）性能优于相关目标（Track 1），推测因极端目标极化、类别不平衡、方言与讽刺细微差别导致。

Conclusion: 共享任务证明当前技术在阿拉伯语立场检测跨目标泛化上取得重大进展，但也揭示相关目标迁移反而更难的反常现象，提示未来研究需关注极化、不平衡、方言讽刺等语言社会因素对泛化能力的影响。

Abstract: StanceEval 2026 is the second edition of the StanceEval shared task series on stance detection in Arabic social media text. Stance detection aims to identify a writer's stance toward a given topic. Given a tweet and a target, participating systems must determine whether the writer's stance is Favor, Against, or None. This edition focuses on cross-target generalization across two distinct evaluation tracks: Track 1 evaluates thematically related cross-target transfer (testing on Women Driving, related to Women Empowerment from training data), while Track 2 evaluates cross-domain transfer to completely unseen targets (E-Cars and Trimester System). The shared task attracted 80 registered teams from 12 countries. During the evaluation phase, 30 unique teams submitted entries, with 21 teams officially ranked in Track 1 and 13 in Track 2 following validation filtering, and 20 teams submitting system-description papers. Participating teams employed diverse methodologies, including fine-tuned pretrained language models, prompt-based and retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs), fine-tuned LLMs, and hybrid cascades. Top systems achieved impressive $F_{avg2}$ scores of 0.8994 on Track 1 and 0.9400 on Track 2, substantially outperforming the strongest baselines (0.7366 and 0.7475, respectively), where $F_{avg2}$ denotes the macro-averaged F1 score over the Favor and Against classes. Counterintuitively, performance on the unseen targets was higher than on the related target, a disparity could be driven by extreme target polarization, class imbalance, and dialectal or sarcastic nuance across topics.

</details>

*Mingzhe Du,Luu Anh Tuan,Xiaobao Wu,Yichong Huang,Yue Liu,Dong Huang,Huijun Liu,Bin Ji,Jie M. Zhang,See-Kiong Ng*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在公共卫生、金融、治理等关键领域部署时需兼顾准确性与社会价值观对齐，但训练数据中的偏见会被模型延续或放大；单模型自我去偏方法依赖内在知识，难以应对根深蒂固的刻板印象。

Conclusion: 集体智能方法对构建更公平LLM具有潜力；委员会拓扑提供了偏见缓解效果与计算开销的实用折中方案，为多模型协作去偏奠定了系统性基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in public health, finance, and governance, requiring both accuracy and societal value alignment. Despite recent advances, LLMs often perpetuate or amplify bias embedded in their training data, posing challenges to fairness. While self-debiasing encourages an LLM to identify and correct its own biases, relying on a single model's intrinsic knowledge may be insufficient to address deeply ingrained stereotypes. To address this limitation, we introduce Collective Bias Mitigation (CBM), a framework that alleviates bias by learning fine-grained model behavior and fostering knowledge sharing among diverse LLMs. This work is the first to systematically explore the effective selection and organization of distinct LLMs to cultivate fairer LLM responses. Experiments show CBM substantially outperforms standalone baselines (e.g., in the top-7 setting, Committee lowers the age bias score from 0.25 to 0.10). Our Debating and Committee topologies achieve substantial bias reduction, with the latter balancing mitigation effectiveness and inference cost, highlighting the potential of CBM for fairer LLMs.

</details>


### [52] [Shrome at Touché: Soft-Vote Ensembling and Counter-Causal Augmentation for Causality Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03268)
*Roham Zendehdel Nobari,Shayan Sooratgar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Touché 2026 将因果抽取扩展到反因果声明，提出三阶段系统（检测、抽取、极性分类），在 CCNC 测试集上达成检测 F1 0.869、极性宏观 F1 0.817、抽取粒度调整 F1 0.728（最高分），证明集成标注器与 LLM 数据增强有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果抽取系统依赖表面线索（如 caused、led to），会将“错误地认为 X 导致 Y”这类反因果句误判为正因果，导致极性错误。需识别并区分 procausal、counter-causal、uncausal 三类极性。

Method: 三个子任务分别建模：1) 检测：微调分类器 + 单条跨任务规则（利用抽取的论元过滤假阳性）。2) 抽取：集成三个 RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF 标注器，在 token 级平均得分后再解码（而非对 span 投票）。3) 极性：因反因果标注稀缺，用 LLM 依 Hagen et al. 的九种反因果表达模式生成训练句，经自动结构检查筛选后加入训练。开发集仅用于组件选择与消融实验。

Result: CCNC 隐藏测试集：检测 F1 0.869；极性分类宏观 F1 0.817；抽取粒度调整 F1 0.728，为所有提交系统（含官方基线）中最高。

Conclusion: 通过集成策略（token 级平均）与针对性 LLM 数据增强，系统成功处理反因果声明，在三子任务上均达最优或接近最优，验证了方法对因果极性细粒度建模的有效性。

Abstract: Touché 2026 extends causality extraction to counter-causal claims: news sentences whose surface form appears causal but whose meaning denies the causation, as in "It is falsely believed that X caused Y." A system that relies on surface cues such as "caused" or "led to" will accept such a sentence as causal and give it the wrong polarity. On the Countercausal News Corpus (CCNC), the task has three subtasks: deciding whether a sentence is causal (detection), locating its cause and effect spans (extraction), and labeling its polarity as procausal, counter-causal, or uncausal. We build one model per subtask. Detection is a fine-tuned classifier with a single cross-task rule that uses the extracted spans to remove false positives. For extraction, we ensemble three RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF taggers by averaging their token-level scores before decoding, rather than voting on the spans each tagger produces. For polarity, where labeled counter-causal examples are scarcest, we add training sentences generated by a large language model prompted with nine patterns of counter-causal expression adapted from Hagen et al., keeping only those that pass automatic structural checks. On the held-out CCNC test set, the system reaches F1 0.869 on detection and macro-F1 0.817 on polarity, and in the organizers' final causal-only evaluation of extraction it scores granularity-adjusted F1 0.728, the highest extraction score among all submissions including the organizers' baseline. The development split is used only for component selection and the ablations reported in the paper.

</details>


### [53] [To Jev or Not? Evaluating the Accuracy and Efficiency of Structured Decision Models for Hate-Speech Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03324)
*Demetris Paschalides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 HATEDECIDE 评估框架，对比六种决策模型配置在四个仇恨言数据集上的表现，研究提供定义和分解问题是否能提升分类性能，发现商业 LLM 仅在一个数据集上显著优于决策模型，提供定义会改变预测但不稳定提升性能，分解问题仅在 20% 比较中显著改善，最佳托管决策模型以约 97% 更低成本接近商业 LLM 性能（宏 F1 差 1.6），表明廉价审核可行但显式标准和额外问题不可靠提升分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线内容规模大，仇恨言审核困难，LLM 使有害内容更易生成，需要高效分类器适应不同仇恨言定义。结构化决策模型接受自然语言标准并选择指定答案，探究其能否在无任务特定训练下满足需求。

Method: 提出 HATEDECIDE 评估框架，测试六种决策模型配置在四个仇恨言数据集上，对比专用审核、零样本、商业和监督基线。考察提供数据集定义或将其分解为多个问题是否改善分类，测量延迟和成本。

Result: 商业 LLM 仅在一个数据集上显著优于所有决策模型。提供定义改变多达 28% 预测但无持续提升分类。分解问题仅在 20% 比较中显著改善性能。诊断测试集上，最佳托管决策模型宏 F1 仅比最佳商业 LLM 低 1.6，推理成本降低约 97%。

Conclusion: 结果揭示廉价审核机会，但显式标准和额外问题不能可靠提升分类性能。决策模型在成本效益上具有优势，但需进一步研究如何有效利用定义和分解策略。

Abstract: The scale of online content makes hate-speech moderation challenging, while Large Language Models (LLMs) enable harmful material to be produced and adapted more easily. Moderation therefore requires efficient classifiers that can accommodate different definitions of hate speech. Recent structured decision models accept natural-language criteria and select among specified answers, raising the question of whether they can meet these requirements without task-specific training. We present HATEDECIDE, an evaluation of six decision-model configurations on four hate-speech datasets against specialized moderation, zero-shot, commercial, and supervised baselines. We examine whether supplying a dataset's definition, or decomposing it into multiple questions, improves classification, and we measure their latency and cost. We find that commercial LLMs significantly outperform all decision models on only one dataset. Supplying definitions changes up to 28\% of predictions without consistently improving classification, and decomposition significantly improves performance in only 20\% of the comparisons. On a diagnostic set of test cases, the best hosted decision model comes within 1.6 macro-F1 points of the best commercial LLM at approximately 97\% lower inference cost. These results identify opportunities for inexpensive moderation, while showing that explicit criteria and additional questions do not reliably improve classification.

</details>


### [54] [SyntaxBench: A Statistical Diagnostic Framework for Character-Level Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03329)
*Mohsen Larni,Sobhan Ebrahimi Azar,Pouyan Nahed,Kazem Taghva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SyntaxBench，一个用于字符级推理的诊断基准和统计评估框架，包含五个核心任务和一个压力测试，评估了八个开放权重模型在多种推理模式下的表现，发现分词方式显著影响准确性，推理模式并非总是有益，且index_to_span任务仍 largely unsolved。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于对微小语法错误敏感的场景，但字符级推理主要通过孤立探针和聚合准确率评估，缺乏受控输入、配对测试以及对分词和推理模式的分析。

Method: 引入SyntaxBench基准，包含字符计数、字母包含、回文检测、编辑距离、最长字符串选择五个核心任务，以及index_to_span子串提取压力测试。使用配对的英语和字符长度匹配的随机字符串输入，以及200-500词文档。采用零样本、一样本、四样本提示。评估八个开放权重模型（2B-32B参数）在11种推理模式配置下的表现。框架报告精确匹配和宽松准确率、Cohen's kappa、配对McNemar检验及优势比、Bootstrap置信区间、Kendall's tau、类条件指标、分词分析及多重比较校正检验。

Result: 三个主要发现：1) 分词塑造准确性：随机字符串比英语字符串更字符可见（1.892 vs 3.169字符/token），英语单词占用更多token时字符计数准确率下降。2) 推理模式非均一有益：Gemma4-31B在近饱和任务上几乎不变；Qwen3.6-27B在回文检测上思考模式反而更差（0.952非思考 vs 0.886思考，四样本）。3) index_to_span仍 largely unsolved，最佳四样本精确匹配准确率仅6.75%。

Conclusion: 字符级评估需要受控输入、配对测试以及对分词和推理模式的分析，而非仅依赖聚合准确率。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used where small syntactic errors matter, yet character-level reasoning is still evaluated mostly through isolated probes and aggregate accuracy. We introduce SyntaxBench, a diagnostic benchmark and statistical evaluation framework for character-level reasoning. It contains five core tasks, character counting, letter containment, palindrome detection, edit distance, and longest-string selection, plus index_to_span, a harder substring-extraction stress test. The five core tasks use paired English and character-length-matched random-string inputs. index_to_span documents share a 200-500 word band and are not character-length matched. All six tasks use zero-, one-, and four-shot prompts.
  We evaluate eight open-weight models from 2B to 32B parameters across 11 reasoning-mode configurations. The framework reports exact-match and relaxed accuracy, Cohen's kappa, paired McNemar tests with odds ratios, bootstrap confidence intervals, Kendall's tau, class-conditional metrics, tokenization analysis, and multiple-comparison-corrected tests. Three findings stand out. First, tokenization shapes accuracy: random strings are more character-visible than English strings (1.892 vs. 3.169 characters per token), and character-counting accuracy falls as English words occupy more tokens. Second, reasoning mode is not uniformly helpful: Gemma4-31B is nearly unchanged across modes on the near-saturated tasks, while Qwen3.6-27B is worse with thinking on palindrome detection (0.952 non-thinking vs. 0.886 thinking at four-shot). Third, index_to_span remains largely unsolved; the best four-shot exact-match accuracy is 6.75%.
  Character-level evaluation needs controlled inputs, paired tests, and analyses of tokenization and reasoning mode rather than aggregate accuracy alone.

</details>


### [55] [CLIMB: Confidence-Guided Complementary Evidence for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03421)
*Hang Gao,Wujiang Xu,Zhixing Zhang,Kai Mei,Jingyi Yang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIMB，一个无需训练的推理时多模态RAG框架，通过互补证据池构建和置信度控制的迭代优化，显著提升知识密集型视觉问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽具备强视觉推理能力，但在知识密集型VQA任务中往往需要图像之外的外部文本证据。现有多模态RAG系统常采用Top-K检索或重排序，易返回冗余段落，且缺乏对答案更新是否被检索证据充分支持的控制机制。

Method: CLIMB包含两阶段：(1) 互补证据池构建——采用MMR风格目标平衡查询相关性与段落级冗余，构建紧凑互补证据池；(2) 置信度控制优化——在固定池内迭代优化：R/E/C评论员从相关性、证据特异性、跨模态对齐三维度评分段落；基于证据的置信度估计器仅在置信度提升时接受更新答案。该设计提供简单停止准则，减少不必要优化，且不修改底层检索器或MLLM。

Result: 在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek基准上，CLIMB持续超越检索增强多模态基线。消融实验表明互补池构建、评论员评分、迭代置信度控制优化三个组件均对最终性能有贡献。

Conclusion: CLIMB作为即插即用的推理时框架，通过互补证据选择与置信度门控机制，有效解决多模态RAG中的证据冗余与答案更新可靠性问题，为知识密集型VQA提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, but knowledge-intensive visual question answering often requires external textual evidence beyond the image and the model's parametric knowledge. Existing multimodal RAG systems commonly rely on Top-$K$ retrieval or reranking, which may return redundant passages and provide limited control over whether an answer update is sufficiently supported by the retrieved evidence. We propose \textit{CLIMB}, a training-free inference-time framework for multimodal RAG. CLIMB first constructs a compact complementary evidence pool using an MMR-style objective that balances query relevance and passage-level redundancy. It then performs confidence-controlled refinement within this fixed pool: an R/E/C critic scores passages by relevance, evidence specificity, and cross-modal alignment, while an evidence-grounded confidence estimator accepts an updated answer only when the estimated confidence increases. This design provides a simple stopping criterion and reduces unnecessary refinement without modifying the underlying retriever or MLLM. Experiments on Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek show that CLIMB consistently improves over retrieval-augmented multimodal baselines. Ablations further indicate that complementary pooling, critic-based scoring, and iterative confidence-controlled refinement each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [56] [Single-Pass Uncertainty Heads for Claim-Level Hallucination Detection in Persian Medical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03482)
*Mehrdad Ghassabi,Pedram Rostami,Hamidreza Baradaran Kashani,Sadra Hakim,Audrina Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将 LLM Uncertainty Head (LUH) 框架适配到基于 Aya-Expanse-8B 的波斯语医学模型 (Gaokerena-V 和 Gaokerena-R)，构建了波斯语声明级幻觉数据集，训练轻量级声明级不确定性头，在单次前向传播中实现幻觉检测，无需检索或重复采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学语言模型的幻觉检测至关重要，但重复采样方法成本高昂，现有不确定性头资源无法直接迁移到新的骨干模型和语言。需要针对波斯语医学模型开发高效的单次前向幻觉检测方法。

Method: 1. 在 168 道伊朗医学入学考试题上评估 Gaokerena-V、Gaokerena-R 与 Aya-Expanse-8B 的五轮一致性；2. 为每个骨干模型构建 1,600 条波斯语声明级配对幻觉数据集；3. 在冻结的骨干模型注意力图和 token 概率上训练轻量级声明级不确定性头；4. 在保留的测试集上评估 PR-AUC 和 ROC-AUC。

Result: Gaokerena-V 五轮一致性显著低于 Aya-Expanse-8B，Gaokerena-R 则相当。训练的不确定性头在两个骨干上分别达到 PR-AUC 0.4820 和 0.4652（为随机基线的 2.30 倍和 2.66 倍），ROC-AUC 分别为 0.7852 和 0.7810。推理时无需检索或重复采样。

Conclusion: 该工作为波斯语医学语言模型的单次前向声明级不确定性估计提供了初步研究；测试集较小且标签为自动生成，结果需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Hallucination detection is particularly important for medical language models, but repeated-sampling approaches are expensive and existing uncertainty-head resources do not directly transfer to a new backbone and language. We adapt the LLM Uncertainty Head (LUH) framework to Aya-Expanse-8B-based Persian medical models, using Gaokerena-V and Gaokerena-R as two previously developed backbones. We first examine response variability on a 168-question Iranian medical entrance examination and observe substantially lower five-run consistency for Gaokerena-V than for Aya-Expanse-8B, whereas Gaokerena-R is comparable to Aya-Expanse-8B. We then construct two paired claim-level hallucination datasets directly in Persian, containing 1,600 responses for each backbone, and train lightweight claim-level heads on frozen backbone attention maps and token probabilities. On held-out test splits, the heads obtain PR-AUCs of 0.4820 and 0.4652, corresponding to 2.30 and 2.66 times their respective random baselines, and ROC-AUCs of 0.7852 and 0.7810. The heads require neither retrieval nor repeated sampling at inference time. These results provide an initial study of single-pass claim-level uncertainty estimation for Persian medical language models; the test splits are small and the labels are automatically generated.

</details>


### [57] [Learning from Repaired Reasoning: Root-Cause-Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03515)
*Chenglei Shen,Haoyang Yao,Weijie Yu,Song Jin,Xiao Zhang,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出根因引导的在线策略蒸馏（RC-OPD），通过诊断和修复学生自身推理中的实质性错误，解决传统参考解蒸馏中的推理错配和蒸馏陷阱问题，在多个数据集和模型规模上显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）使用参考解作为事后指导，存在两个核心问题：1）推理错配——参考解只解释正确答案，不针对学生自身推理失败的原因；2）蒸馏陷阱——对整条轨迹统一施加事后约束，会对有效推理施加不必要限制，干扰实质性错误的修正。

Method: RC-OPD 采用迭代诊断-修复-续写流程：对每次失败尝试，定位最早实质性错误，开发局部修正，将修正后的中间结果作为有效前缀的锚点；在固定修复预算内让学生继续推理以测试修复；若修复链到达正确答案，则用根因引导蒸馏（失败诊断和修正目标）监督错误片段，用锚点引导蒸馏（通向修复中间结果的推理链）支持对应有效前缀。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型规模上的大量实验和分析表明，RC-OPD 有效缓解推理错配和蒸馏陷阱，带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: RC-OPD 通过利用学生自身推理的修复而非外部参考解，实现了针对性错误纠正与有效推理保留的平衡，为在线策略蒸馏提供了更精准、高效的指导范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses reference solutions as privileged hindsight to supervise student-generated reasoning trajectories. However, reference-based guidance may explain a correct solution without addressing why the student's own reasoning fails. This reasoning mismatch between the guidance provided and the correction needed can encourage the student to borrow correct conclusions while leaving its reasoning errors unresolved. Moreover, applying the same hindsight throughout the trajectory risks a distillation trap, where unnecessary constraints on valid reasoning compete with correction of substantive errors. To address these issues, we propose Root-Cause-Guided On-Policy Distillation (RC-OPD), which uses repairs of the student's own reasoning to provide guidance that addresses its specific errors while building on valid progress. For each failed attempt, RC-OPD locates the earliest substantive error, develops a local correction, and uses the corrected intermediate result as an anchor for the valid prefix. An iterative diagnosis--repair--continuation process tests the repairs through student continuation, identifying further errors within a fixed repair budget. For repair chains that reach a correct answer, root--cause--guided distillation uses failure diagnoses and corrective goals to supervise the erroneous segments, while anchor-guided distillation supports the corresponding valid prefixes with reasoning chains leading to the repaired intermediate results. We evaluate RC-OPD across multiple datasets and model scales. Extensive experiments and analyses show that it mitigates reasoning mismatch and the distillation trap, yielding substantial performance gains.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

Abstract: Table-to-report generation refers to the task of automatically generating article-level analyt- ical reports from relational tables and is an essential capability for automated data science and decision support. Its central challenge lies in systematically discovering verifiable com- posite insights across tables, attributes, and analytical perspectives, and organizing them into coherent, complete, and traceable evidence chains. Existing methods primarily rely on sequential, reactive data agents or direct Large Language Model(LLM) generation. They suffer from exploration bias: early local observations constrain subsequent actions, causing models to focus prematurely on local analyzes and miss cross-table or cross-dimensional evidence. We propose ComInsight, which reformulates insight discovery as the composition of atomic evidences. We first define an atomic insight as the smallest executable analytical unit conforming to a predefined analysis pattern and enumerate all valid atomic insights from database schema and content. These atoms are then organized into a multi-relational insight graph, where nodes represent verified data facts and edges encode logical, temporal, or hierarchical relations. Finally, a set of composition operators systematically fuses atomic nodes into higher-order composite conclusions. Every composite output is accompanied by executable SQL and fine-grained provenance, ensuring full verifiability. Across three benchmarks InsightBench, DDR-Bench, and T2R-Bench, ComInsight consistently outperforms strong baselines in factual correctness, novelty, and structural completeness. We believe ComInsight offers a reliable, efficient, and explainable path toward table-to-report generation.

</details>


### [59] [Author Representation Strategies for Zero-Shot Authorship Attribution: A Comparative Study of LLM-Based and Embedding-Based Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03531)
*Nudrat Habib,Tosin Adewumi,Sana Sabah Al-Azzawi,Marcus Liwicki,Elisa Barney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究零样本作者归因（ZS AA）中作者表征的效果，对比了仅标签提示、代表性写作样本、LLM生成描述和风格嵌入（LISA）四种策略，提出两阶段LISA框架结合候选空间缩减与嵌入维度选择，实验表明仅标签提示无效，作者表征显著提升性能，两阶段LISA表现最优，LLM生成描述虽紧凑但性能略逊，说明当前开源LLM在无更有效表征学习下难以稳健归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者归因需要捕捉细粒度文体特征，在无任务特定监督的零样本设置下尤为困难，现有方法缺乏对作者表征策略的系统性对比与优化。

Method: 评估四种作者表征策略：1）仅标签提示基线；2）代表性写作样本；3）LLM生成风格描述；4）风格嵌入（LISA），前三者基于LLM提示归因，后者用余弦相似度。并研究提示设计影响，提出两阶段嵌入式归因框架，结合候选空间缩减与嵌入维度选择。

Result: 仅标签零样本AA无效；引入作者特定表征一致提升性能；两阶段LISA框架整体最优；LLM生成描述提供更紧凑表征但牺牲部分性能。

Conclusion: 作者表征在零样本AA中至关重要，但当前开源LLM若无更有效表征学习仍不足以实现稳健归因。

Abstract: Authorship Attribution (AA) requires capturing fine-grained stylistic characteristics, making it particularly challenging in zero-shot (ZS) settings where no task-specific supervision is available. In this work, we investigate the effect of author representations on ZS AA by evaluating a label-only prompting baseline together with three author representation strategies: representative writing samples, LLM-generated descriptions, and style embeddings (LISA). The first three approaches perform attribution using LLM prompting, while the embedding-based approach uses style embeddings with cosine similarity. We investigate the influence of prompt design and propose a two-stage embedding-based attribution framework that combines candidate space reduction with embedding-dimension selection. The results show that label-only ZS AA is ineffective, while incorporating author-specific representations consistently improves attribution performance. Among the evaluated approaches, the proposed two-stage LISA framework achieves the strongest overall performance, whereas LLM-generated style descriptions provide a substantially more compact representation of author style at the cost of some attribution performance. These findings demonstrate the importance of author representation in ZS AA, while indicating that current open-source LLMs remain insufficient for robust attribution without more effective representation learning.

</details>


### [60] [Writerslogic at PAN 2026: Process over Content for Robust Detection under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03565)
*David L. Condrey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个统一的分析框架，解释特征在分布偏移下的鲁棒性取决于训练与测试分布的支撑重叠，而非训练集效应量。基于此分类（域锚定、域可迁移、域不变），设计了三个PAN 2026共享任务的系统：推理轨迹检测获源头检测第1名（0.85宏F1）和安全分类第3名（0.66宏F1）；Voight-Kampff任务校准集成达0.891；多作者写作风格分析因平台问题未参与官方评测。核心结论：测量生成过程属性的特征在域偏移下优于测量生成内容属性的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征工程在分布偏移下失效，缺乏理论指导。作者观察到生成器特定特征在域偏移下失效，而词汇指纹、压缩度量和字符n-gram存活，旨在建立统一理论框架解释这一现象，并指导跨三个PAN 2026任务的系统设计。

Method: 核心方法：1）理论框架：特征鲁棒性由训练-测试分布支撑重叠决定，非训练集效应量；建立域锚定/域可迁移/域不变三级分类。2）推理轨迹检测：Opus-Sonnet一致性用于源头检测，查询-拒绝分解用于安全分类。3）Voight-Kampff：DeBERTa-v2(ONNX)+多种子LightGBM(44个域可迁移文体特征)+SVM(n-gram TF-IDF)，通过学习堆叠与等渗校准集成。4）多作者写作风格分析：字符n-gram相似度图谱谱聚类+归一化压缩距离局部边界检测+SmolLM-135M困惑度神经变点检测融合。

Result: 1）推理轨迹检测：源头检测宏F1 0.85获第1名；安全分类宏F1 0.66获第3名（训练全数学，测试84%未见域）。2）Voight-Kampff：测试集0.891，子指标均衡（0.853-0.902跨所有评估维度）。3）多作者写作风格分析：因平台混淆未参与官方评测，仅报告设计与先验预测。

Conclusion: 统一框架证实：测量生成过程属性（如词汇指纹、压缩度量、字符n-gram）的域不变/域可迁移特征，在分布偏移下显著优于测量生成内容属性的域锚定特征（如生成器特定特征）。该理论指导的特征选择与系统设计在三个异构任务中均取得竞争性结果，验证了框架的普适性与实用性。

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic systems for three PAN at CLEF 2026 shared tasks (Reasoning Trajectory Detection, Voight-Kampff Generative AI Detection, and Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis), unified by a shared analytical framework: feature robustness under distribution shift is governed by support overlap between training and test distributions, not by training-set effect size. This yields a taxonomy (domain-anchored, domain-portable, domain-invariant) that explains why generator-specific features die under domain shift while vocabulary fingerprints (hapax ratio, Yule's K, Heaps' exponent), compression measures, and character n-grams survive. On Reasoning Trajectory Detection, where training was entirely mathematics and 84 percent of test was unseen domains, the framework guided system design to 1st place in source detection (0.85 macro F1 via Opus-Sonnet agreement) and 3rd place in safety classification (0.66 macro F1 via query-refusal decomposition). For Voight-Kampff, we built a calibrated ensemble of DeBERTa-v2 (ONNX), multi-seed LightGBM with 44 domain-portable stylometric features, and SVM on n-gram TF-IDF, combined via learned stacking with isotonic calibration; the best configuration achieved 0.891 on the PAN 2026 test set with balanced sub-metrics (0.853 to 0.902 across all evaluation dimensions). For Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis, we describe a system fusing spectral clustering over character n-gram similarity graphs, normalized compression distance for local boundary detection, and SmolLM-135M perplexity for neural change-point detection; a platform mix-up meant our run never reached the official evaluation, so we report the design and its a priori predictions. Across all three tasks, features measuring generation process properties are designed to outperform features measuring generated content properties under domain shift.

</details>

*David L. Condrey*

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic team's participation in the CLEF 2026 SimpleText shared task, addressing Task 1 (text simplification) and Task 2 (complexity spotting). For Task 1, we develop a multi-candidate generation pipeline using GPT-4o-mini that produces five simplification candidates per sentence at varying temperatures, then selects the best candidate using a reference-free scoring heuristic that rewards compression, source word retention, Cochrane Plain Language Summary vocabulary usage, and lexical simplicity. On Task 1.1 (sentence-level simplification), our Claude Sonnet 4 submission achieves SARI 47.43 and BLEU 14.21, the top-ranked sentence-level system (3rd on the combined Task 1 leaderboard, behind two document-level submissions). For Task 2, we fine-tune a DeBERTa-v3-large NLI model on 350K labeled (source, sentence) pairs, framing hallucination detection as natural language inference. The model reads the most relevant source sentence as premise and the candidate as hypothesis, directly learning to distinguish grounded from hallucinated content. On Task 2.1 (binary overgeneration identification), our fine-tuned DeBERTa system achieves 0.8081 document-level macro F1 (0.8085 in our best ensemble), the top-ranked entry within the identification track and 2nd among teams overall, behind AIIR Lab (0.8197). On Task 2.2 (multi-class error classification), our best submission reaches 0.804 multiclass accuracy, ranking 2nd among unique teams behind AIIR Lab (0.827). We evaluate both tasks on English and multilingual biomedical text from Cochrane systematic reviews.

</details>


### [62] [FALCON: A Model and Dataset Agnostic Framework for Synthetic Data Generation for NL2SQL Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03625)
*Darian Lee,Shannon Rumsey,Jack St. Clair,Xinyi Tang,Aditya Bansal,Yuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FALCON是一个生成真实、歧义感知的NL-to-SQL训练数据的框架，通过保留字SQL种子和基于人设的提示生成结构复杂查询，利用对齐过滤保持难度，生成的数据在SQL复杂度和自然语言丰富度上超越现有基准，训练模型在复杂查询上表现更优，且支持本地部署无需外部API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成NL-to-SQL数据生成方法忽视自然语言中的歧义，产生过度简化的查询，无法让模型应对真实世界结构化知识访问的复杂性。

Method: FALCON框架结合保留字SQL种子和基于人设的提示生成结构复杂的查询，采用对齐过滤区分真正错误示例与复杂但有效的查询，从而保留难度。该方法模型无关、数据库无关，支持本地低成本生成。

Result: 人工评估确认跨模型尺寸的一致高质量；生成数据在SQL复杂度和自然语言丰富度上超越现有基准。难度分层分析显示，在FALCON数据上训练的模型随查询复杂度增加越来越优于基线模型。混合少量基准数据可恢复简单查询性能，同时保持复杂查询优势。

Conclusion: FALCON实现了低成本、本地化生成高复杂度NL-to-SQL训练数据，有效解决了歧义感知和查询复杂度不足的问题，为真实世界部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Relational databases are among the most widely deployed forms of structured knowledge, and natural language access to them requires grounding language onto schema entities and relations while handling the ambiguity inherent in how people phrase requests. Existing synthetic NL-to-SQL data generation methods largely ignore this ambiguity and produce oversimplified queries that fail to prepare models for the complexity of real-world structured knowledge access. We present FALCON, a framework that generates realistic, ambiguity-aware NL-to-SQL data matching the complexity of challenging real-world benchmarks, at low cost using compact open models. Our approach combines reserved-word SQL seeding and persona-based prompting to generate structurally complex queries, while alignment-based filtering preserves difficulty by distinguishing genuinely incorrect examples from complex but valid queries. Human evaluation confirms consistent high quality across model sizes, and our generated data exceeds existing benchmarks in both SQL complexity and natural language richness. Difficulty-stratified analysis shows models trained on FALCON data increasingly outperform baseline-trained models as query complexity increases, validating our pipeline's success in generating challenging training data. When combined with a small proportion of existing benchmark data, mixed training recovers performance on simpler queries while preserving these advantages on complex ones. The model- and database-agnostic design enables organizations to generate high-complexity NL-to-SQL training data locally without external APIs.

</details>


### [63] [Language Models that Play Chess and Explain Their Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03695)
*Adithya Bhaskar,Jeffrey Cheng,Danqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Queen，一个40亿参数的国际象棋语言模型，能够在大师级水平下解释走法和计划。通过编码器-解码器架构和迭代蒸馏算法，结合静默专家国际象棋编码器与指令调优语言模型，实现领域特定推理。经过7次迭代，模型Elo分数提升900多点（1782至2697），在棋力和谜题准确性上超越前沿模型，且参数量少三个数量级。解释流畅且连贯性接近GPT-5.6-Sol。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代国际象棋引擎虽棋力超人但无法提供解释；语言模型虽能生成合理解释但棋力弱，限制了解释效用。需要结合两者优势：既有高水平棋力又能生成高质量自然语言解释。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构，将静默专家国际象棋编码器与指令调优语言模型通过交叉注意力整合。通过问答课程训练提取国际象棋概念。在此基础上，使用自然语言版贝尔曼更新进行迭代蒸馏：模型分析其顶级候选走法后的局面，将其整合为当前局面的解释，再蒸馏回模型。共进行7次迭代。

Result: 经过7次迭代，模型Elo分数从1782提升至2697（增益900+），在棋力和谜题准确性上大幅超越所有前沿模型，尽管参数量少三个数量级。基于语言模型的评估显示，生成的解释流畅且连贯性接近GPT-5.6-Sol（高）。

Conclusion: 该架构和训练过程的通用性表明，对于拥有静默专家编码器的领域（如游戏、机器人、计算机使用），可将语言模型应用于此类领域的通用配方。

Abstract: Modern chess engines are silent experts: they play at a superhuman level, but do not offer explanations for their play. On the other hand, language models (LMs) can generate plausible-sounding explanations, but their weak playing strength limits the utility of their explanations. We introduce Queen, a 4B-parameter chess-language model that can explain its moves and plans while playing at the level of a typical Grandmaster. Our novel framework enables domain-specific reasoning through complementary components: an encoder-decoder architecture and an iterative distillation algorithm. This architecture integrates a silent expert chess encoder with an instruction-tuned LM through cross-attention, which we train via a question-answering curriculum to extract chess concepts from the encoder's representations. Building on this domain-adapted model, we iteratively improve its explanations with a natural-language analog of the Bellman update: the model analyzes the positions after its top candidate moves and consolidates them into an explanation of the current position, which is then distilled back into the model. Over seven iterations, our model gains over 900 Elo points (1782 to 2697), substantially surpassing all frontier models on both playing strength and puzzle accuracy, despite containing three orders of magnitude fewer parameters. Furthermore, LM-based evaluations show that our explanations are fluent and approach GPT-5.6-Sol (high) in coherence. The generality of our architecture and training procedure suggests a recipe for applying language models to domains where silent expert encoders are available, like games, robotics, and computer use.

</details>

### [64] [Adaptive Sparsity Optimization with Learnable Soft Top-K and Per-Term Thresholding for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02572)
*Wentai Xie,Parker Carlson,Shanxiu He,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应稀疏化方案，结合可学习soft top-K、逐词阈值化和FLOPs正则化，显著降低神经稀疏检索中查询和文档向量的长度，大幅缩短检索延迟和降低存储成本，同时保持高竞争力的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经稀疏检索虽利用LLM语义词扩展取得强相关性，但现有稀疏化技术因LLM词表大导致向量过长，严重制约检索时空效率。

Method: 提出协同自适应策略：可学习soft top-K选择重要词项、逐词阈值化动态过滤低权重词、FLOPs正则化约束计算量，联合优化查询和文档向量稀疏度。

Result: 在MS MARCO和BEIR数据集上，Lion-SP模型采用该方案显著降低平均查询/文档长度，检索延迟和存储成本大幅下降，相关性指标保持高竞争力。

Conclusion: 该自适应稀疏化方案有效解决神经稀疏检索向量过长问题，实现高效检索与高相关性的平衡，为大规模稀疏检索提供实用优化路径。

Abstract: Recent work on neural sparse retrieval has demonstrated strong relevance by leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic term expansion. However, learned models paired with previous sparsification techniques still yield overly long document and query vectors partly due to a large LLM vocabulary, imposing a serious challenge to retrieval time and space efficiency. This paper proposes a scheme for optimizing model sparsity through a synergy of adaptive strategies, including learnable soft top-K, per-term thresholding, and FLOPs regularization to increase the sparsity of query and document vectors. Experimental results with Lion-SP model on the MS MARCO and BEIR datasets demonstrate that the proposed scheme can outperform the baselines by significantly reducing the average query and document lengths. Our scheme can achieve much shorter retrieval latency and lower storage cost while maintaining highly competitive relevance.

</details>


### [65] [When History Misleads: Asymmetric Margin Supervision for Instruction-Guided LLM Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02600)
*Ming Yin,Yuhan Yang,Chen Chen,Xinyu Lin,Wentao Shi,Fangcong Yin,Chaofei Yang,Chao Yang,Jiyan Yang,Hui Zhang,Ning Jiang,Yiran Chen,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Asymmetric Intervention-Guided Margin Supervision (AIMS) 方法，解决指令引导生成式推荐中 LLM 推荐器平衡当前请求与历史偏好的冲突问题。通过冻结参考模型识别能同时提升目标物品得分及其对竞品优势的历史删除操作，将这些边际作为训练目标，结合交叉熵与不对称辅助损失训练，推理阶段无需历史编辑。实验显示在六种 LLM 主干、工业数据集及两个公开基准上均提升 Recall 和 NDCG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在指令引导生成式推荐中，LLM 推荐器需平衡用户当前请求与交互历史偏好。当两者冲突时，历史事件可能覆盖请求。将单个历史事件的影响转化为监督面临两大障碍：(1) 对推荐影响最大的事件未必支持目标物品；(2) 移除误导性事件虽能提升目标得分，但可能更大幅提升竞品得分，导致排名不升反降。现有方法难以有效利用历史事件级别的干预信号进行排序监督。

Method: 提出 AIMS 方法：(1) 对已正确排序的训练请求，使用冻结参考模型搜索请求特定的历史删除操作，筛选出能同时提升目标物品得分及其对临近推荐截断位置竞品的边际优势的删除集合；(2) 将这些边际作为训练目标，保留完整历史作为输入；(3) 训练损失结合交叉熵与不对称辅助损失，后者惩罚边际不足且仅将梯度路由至竞品得分；(4) 推理阶段保持不变，无需历史编辑或删除搜索。

Result: 在工业数据集和两个公开基准上，跨六种 LLM 主干模型，AIMS 在 Recall 和 NDCG 指标上均显著优于强基线。消融实验验证了请求特定边际与不对称监督的有效性，且选中的删除操作倾向于移除违反约束的历史事件。

Conclusion: AIMS 通过不对称干预引导的边际监督，有效将历史事件级别的干预效应转化为排序监督信号，解决了指令引导生成式推荐中请求与历史冲突的核心难题。该方法无需修改推理流程，兼容性强，在多种 LLM 主干和数据集上均展现出稳健的性能提升，为生成式推荐中的历史利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: In instruction-guided generative recommendation, LLM-based recommenders need to balance two goals: responding to the user's current request and aligning with the preferences in their interaction history. When the two conflict, history events can override the request. We show that turning the effect of individual history events into supervision faces two obstacles. First, the events that most influence a recommendation are not necessarily the ones that support the target item. Second, removing a misleading event can raise the target's score but a competing item's score even more, so a higher target score alone does not guarantee a better ranking. We propose Asymmetric Intervention-Guided Margin Supervision (AIMS), which converts the effect of removing individual history events into ranking supervision. For training requests already ranked correctly, a frozen reference model identifies request-specific deletions that improve both the target's score and its margin over a competitor near the recommendation cutoff. These margins serve as training targets, while the complete history is retained as input. Training combines cross-entropy with an asymmetric auxiliary loss that penalizes margin shortfalls and routes its gradient only through the competitor score. Inference is unchanged, requiring no history editing or deletion search. Across six LLM backbones on an industrial dataset and two public benchmarks, AIMS improves Recall and NDCG over strong baselines. Ablations support request-specific margins and asymmetric supervision, and the selected deletions preferentially remove constraint-violating history.

</details>


### [66] [Learning Query Encoders Can Be Hard Even When Vector Retrieval Is Geometrically Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02749)
*Anders Wikum,Nina Mishra,Amin Saberi,Tal Wagner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究向量检索中查询编码器的学习难度，发现真实基准上单向量查询编码器的召回率远低于文档索引的几何容量上限，并从理论上证明学习最优查询编码器在统计查询模型下需要指数级查询复杂度，揭示了查询编码器可学习性可能是嵌入检索的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注文档语料库的几何容量（如实现所有 top-k 答案集所需的最小嵌入维度），但忽视了在冻结文档索引下查询编码器能达到的最大召回率。作者观察到真实检索基准上单向量查询编码器的表现远低于文档索引的理论上限，因此想探究这是否源于查询编码器学习的计算难度。

Method: 1. 实证分析：在多个真实检索基准上测量单向量查询编码器的召回率与文档索引几何容量上限的差距。2. 理论构造：设计一个检索任务，其中存在由小型单隐藏层 ReLU 网络表示的完美召回查询编码器。3. 复杂度下界证明：在统计查询（SQ）学习模型下，证明任何统计查询学习者要获得非平凡召回优势（优于随机基线 k/n），需要指数级数量的统计查询。

Result: 实验表明真实基准上单向量查询编码器的检索质量显著低于文档索引的几何容量上限。理论上构造了存在完美召回编码器（小型 ReLU 网络可表示）但 SQ 学习者需指数级查询才能超越随机基线的检索任务，确立了查询编码器学习的计算难度。

Conclusion: 检索基准中存在大量未被利用的几何容量，查询编码器的可学习性是嵌入式检索的一个潜在根本障碍。单纯增加模型容量或数据规模可能不足以弥合这一差距，需要新的算法或训练范式来克服统计查询学习的固有局限。

Abstract: Efficient vector retrieval requires both a corpus geometry that supports retrieving the right documents through vector similarity, and a query encoder that can embed queries near their desired documents in the embedding space. Recent work has studied geometric capacity through the lens of the minimum embedding dimension needed to realize all top-$k$ answer sets of $n$ documents. We study a different notion of geometric capacity--the maximum recall achievable for a frozen document index--and explore whether learned query encoders can reach this ceiling. On several real-world retrieval benchmarks, we show that retrieval quality of single-vector query encoders often lies far below what the document indices can support.
  Motivated by this observation, we give theoretical evidence that learning query encoders can be computationally hard. In particular, we construct a retrieval task that (1) admits a query encoder with perfect recall which is representable by a small one-hidden-layer ReLU network, but (2) any statistical-query learner (a class capturing learners that access training data through aggregate statistics) provably requires exponentially many statistical queries to achieve non-trivial recall advantage over the random baseline $k/n$. Taken together, our results suggest substantial unrealized geometric capacity in retrieval benchmarks and establish query encoder learnability as a possible barrier in embedding-based retrieval.

</details>


### [67] [Benchmarking Literature Retrieval for a Model Organism: A Dictyostelium Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03130)
*Yun Wang,Gad Shaulsky,Tomaž Curk,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个针对Dictyostelium（一种模式生物）的生物文献检索基准dictyBase，并研究了三种因素在利基生物检索中的效果：交叉编码器重排序、基因感知查询扩展以及仅摘要与全文检索的对比。结果显示：重排序在模型适合生物证据匹配时最有效；基因注释能减少词汇不匹配，澄清简短查询；全文分块在摘要缺乏支持证据时显著提升召回和顶层性能，但此类查询本身更难。数据与代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物文献检索系统多基于广泛的生物医学语料库和通用搜索任务开发评估，但许多精心维护的知识库运行在较窄的模式生物领域，文献稀疏且术语具有生物特异性，缺乏针对性的评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含策展人生成的生物查询、链接到PubMed索引文章以及结构化基因注释的检索基准。在此基准上系统评估三种技术：交叉编码器重排序、基因感知查询扩展、以及仅使用摘要与使用全文分块进行检索的对比。

Result: 重排序和基因感知查询扩展有选择地提升检索效果：重排序在模型擅长生物证据匹配时效果最好；基因注释通过减少词汇不匹配帮助澄清紧凑的生物查询；全文分块在摘要遗漏支持证据时显著提高候选召回率和顶层排名性能，尽管这些查询比摘要支持的查询更难。

Conclusion: 针对利基模式生物领域的文献检索，结合领域特异性注释（基因感知扩展）和全文证据能有效缓解词汇不匹配和证据缺失问题；交叉编码器重排序的效用取决于模型对生物证据匹配的适配程度。该基准为专业生物知识库的检索系统评估提供了宝贵资源。

Abstract: Biological literature retrieval systems are often developed and evaluated using broad biomedical corpora and general-purpose search tasks. However, many curated knowledge bases operate in narrower model-organism domains, where the literature is sparse and terminology is organism-specific. We introduce a retrieval benchmark from dictyBase for Dictyostelium, a model organism in cell and developmental biology. The benchmark consists of curator-generated biological queries linked to PubMed-indexed articles, together with structured gene annotations. Using this benchmark, we study three factors in niche biological retrieval: cross-encoder reranking, gene-aware query expansion, and abstract-only versus full-text retrieval. We report that reranking and gene-aware query expansion improve retrieval selectively: reranking is most useful when the model is well suited to biological evidence matching, whereas curated annotations help clarify compact biological queries by reducing vocabulary mismatch. Full-text chunks substantially improve retrieval when abstracts omit supporting evidence, increasing both candidate recall and top-rank performance, although these cases are harder than queries supported by abstracts. Data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/fulaibaowang/dictycite, and the benchmark dataset is additionally archived on Zenodo.

</details>

### [68] [Hybrid Machine Learning-Assisted Raman Spectroscopy with Generative Feature Augmentation for Pharmaceutical Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02224)
*Quach Thi Thai Binh,Ton Nu Quynh Trang,Thang B. Phan,Vu Thi Hanh Thu,Nguyen Tuan Hung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 HyMLRaman 框架，结合深度光谱特征提取、生成模型和传统机器学习分类器，实现六种药物残留的快速拉曼光谱筛选，基线模型 EfficientNet-B3+SVM 达到 96.31% 准确率，DDPM 特征增强在小样本条件下对 KNN 等分类器有选择性提升，并演示了可交互的拉曼药物分析器应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速可靠地识别药物残留对公共卫生、食品安全和基于拉曼的实用筛选至关重要，现有方法在特征提取、小样本泛化和部署落地方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 将拉曼光谱转为光谱图像，用 EfficientNet-B3 等深度骨干网络提取 1536 维嵌入，随后训练 SVM、KNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、ANN 等下游分类器，采用分层 10 折交叉验证；针对小样本场景，在 PCA 降维的 EfficientNet-B3 潜在空间引入 DDPM 生成模型进行特征增强，并开发交互式拉曼药物分析器验证部署可行性。

Result: EfficientNet-B3+SVM 配置取得最优基线性能：准确率 96.31%，宏平均 F1 96.36%，显著优于单独 CNN 基线；DDPM 增强在减少训练样本比例时对 KNN 等分类器有选择性提升，效果依赖于分类器类型；应用级分析器证明了模型可嵌入实际拉曼分析工作流。

Conclusion: HyMLRaman 通过混合深度特征提取、生成增强和经典分类器，提供了一条实用且可解释的快速拉曼药物筛选路径，兼顾高性能、小样本鲁棒性和部署落地。

Abstract: Rapid and reliable identification of pharmaceutical residues is important for safeguarding public health, ensuring food safety, and enabling practical Raman-based screening. In this study, we propose HyMLRaman, a hybrid Raman spectroscopy framework that combines deep spectral feature extraction, generative models, and classical machine-learning classifiers to identify six pharmaceutical compounds, including amoxicillin, chloramphenicol, ciprofloxacin, tetracycline, ibuprofen, and paracetamol. Raman spectra are converted into spectral images and encoded with several deep neural-network backbones, among which EfficientNet-B3 yields the most effective representation. The resulting 1536-dimensional embeddings are then used to train downstream classifiers, including SVM, KNN, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, and ANN, using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The hybrid EfficientNet-B3--SVM configuration achieves the strongest baseline performance, reaching 96.31% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.36%, outperforming the standalone CNN baseline. To address limited-data conditions, a generative model, a DDPM-based feature augmentation, is introduced in a PCA-reduced EfficientNet-B3 latent space. The low-data ablation results show that DDPM augmentation provides selective benefits, particularly for KNN with reduced training fractions, and that its effect remains classifier-dependent. Finally, an application-level Raman Pharmaceutical Analyzer demonstrates the feasibility of embedding the trained model into an interactive Raman analysis workflow. These results suggest that HyMLRaman provides a practical and interpretable route for rapid Raman-based pharmaceutical screening.

</details>


### [69] [The Price of Greenwashing: Algorithmic Verification and Market Discipline using Conformal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02225)
*Sourav Bose,Taoufik Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新颖的CWCD指标，通过融合SEC财务数据和EPA温室气体登记册，利用梯度提升和Mondrian共形预测量化企业自报排放与算法基线的偏差，发现排放偏差与后续市场估值和盈利能力显著负相关，证明市场对环境欺骗进行定价，为算法审计提供量化依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业可持续发展强制要求扩大，但依赖自报排放数据导致漂绿风险；现有文献依赖主观ESG评级或文本情感分析，缺乏客观量化物理气候现实的计量经济学方法，存在信息不对称。

Method: 融合美国SEC财务基本面与设施级EPA温室气体登记册，建立物理企业排放的数学保证基线；采用梯度提升架构和Mondrian共形预测，量化自报数据与算法基线的差距，构建共形加权连续散度（CWCD）指标；采用横截面领滞计量经济学设计评估散度影响。

Result: 算法排放散度与后续市场估值（Tobin's Q）和运营盈利能力（ROA）呈显著负相关；制度性资本将环境欺骗定价为基本面管理不善的领先指标，而非仅是道德失误；反驳了市场盲目性假设。

Conclusion: 研究为资产管理者和监管者大规模部署算法审计基础设施提供了量化理由。

Abstract: While corporate sustainability mandates are expanding, the systemic reliance on self-reported emissions data exposes financial markets to pervasive greenwashing. Current literature relies heavily on subjective ESG ratings or textual sentiment analysis, leaving a critical econometric gap in objectively quantifying physical climate realities. To resolve this information asymmetry, we fuse U.S. SEC financial fundamentals with facility-level EPA greenhouse gas registries to establish a mathematically guaranteed baseline of physical corporate emissions. Leveraging a gradient boosting architecture and Mondrian Conformal Prediction, we quantify the shortfall between self-reported data and this algorithmic baseline into a novel Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence (CWCD) metric. Evaluating this divergence via a cross-sectional lead-lag econometric design, we uncover a robust mechanism of market discipline: algorithmic emissions divergence exhibits a severe, statistically significant negative relationship with subsequent market valuation (Tobin's Q) and operational profitability (ROA). Providing definitive evidence against the market blindness hypothesis, this study proves that institutional capital actively prices environmental deception not merely as an ethical lapse, but as a leading indicator of fundamental corporate mismanagement. Ultimately, these findings provide the quantitative justification necessary for asset managers and regulators to deploy algorithmic auditing infrastructure at scale.

</details>


### [70] [State-Space Unlearning for Non-Stationary Bias in Land Surface Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02248)
*Anidipta Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SSU-LSF（面向土地表面预报的状态空间遗忘框架），首个专为地学领域Mamba类结构化状态空间模型（SSM）设计的机器遗忘方法，旨在消除非平稳混杂事件（如灌溉激增、大坝调度变更、传感器重校准）被隐式吸收进状态转移矩阵后持续偏置NDVI、LST及作物物候预测的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Mamba族SSM的操作性土地表面预报系统会将非平稳混杂事件隐式编码进状态转移矩阵，导致NDVI、LST及作物物候预测在物理原因结束后长期存在偏置，现有方法缺乏针对此类地学SSM的针对性遗忘机制。

Method: 1) 基于EKFac影响函数，通过闭式矩阵指数梯度特化Mamba状态矩阵；2) 利用谱半径加权肘部阈值定位时序混杂足迹Φ；3) 在KL散度信任域内施行海森堡自由投影梯度上升，并引入空间全变分（TV）正则化约束。

Result: 在CropHarvest、NDVI-LST、ERA5三大异构基准上对比十一基线，混杂消除率分别达0.773、0.821、0.859；最坏情况净域RMSE退化仅4.2%（ERA5）；单次遗忘请求收敛需3-5个epoch，GPU成本较完全重训练降低8.4倍。

Conclusion: SSU-LSF为地学Mamba-SSM提供了首个理论保证（残留混杂界为O((1-ρ(Ā)^Tc)/((1-ρ(Ā))μ))）且计算高效的机器遗忘方案，有效解决了非平稳混杂事件对长序列预测的持久污染问题。

Abstract: Operational land surface forecasting systems built on Mamba-family Structured State Space Models absorb non-stationary confounding events (unrecorded irrigation booms, dam-operation shifts, sensor recalibrations) into their state-transition matrices, silently biasing NDVI, LST, and crop phenology predictions long after the physical cause ends. This paper introduces SSU-LSF (State-Space Unlearning for Land Surface Forecasting), the first machine-unlearning framework purpose-built for geoscientific Mamba-based SSMs. We develop EKFac influence functions specialized to the Mamba state matrices via a closed-form matrix-exponential gradient, use spectral-radius-weighted elbow thresholding to localize a temporal confounding footprint $Φ$, and apply Hessian-free projected gradient ascent within a KL-divergence trust region augmented by spatial total-variation (TV) regularization. Proposition 1 establishes that residual confounding is bounded by $\mathcal{O}\big((1-ρ(\bar{A})^{T_c})/((1-ρ(\bar{A}))μ)\big)$, which grows with the window length $T_c$. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks and eleven baselines, SSU-LSF achieves confounding reduction rates of $0.773$ (CropHarvest), $0.821$ (NDVI-LST), and $0.859$ (ERA5), with worst-case clean-domain RMSE degradation of $4.2\%$ on ERA5, converging in 3--5 epochs at $8.4\times$ lower GPU-cost per unlearning request than full retraining. Code: https://github.com/Anidipta/SSU-LSF

</details>


### [71] [A Manifold-Aware Topic Modeling Approach via Rank-Based Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29630)
*Thiago César Castilho Almeida,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARETopic 是一个无需训练的主题发现框架，通过在低维流形上投影嵌入并构建有序邻域列表，采用贪心算法选择 K 个真实语料文档作为典范原型，两种变体在纯度、NMI 和主题连贯性上达最优，且无需任何梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练嵌入的主题模型存在两大缺陷：神经架构生成的潜在表示缺乏具体文本支撑；聚类流水线事后分配代表文档，依赖高维空间中受中心性和各向异性扭曲的绝对距离，导致主题发现不可靠。

Method: 将嵌入投影到低维流形，构建编码有序邻域结构的排序列表；贪心算法选取恰好 K 个真实语料文档作为典范，使其邻域覆盖全语料。MARETopic_Corr 结合查询性能预测器与秩相关度量评分；MARETopic_Diff 使用基于秩的扩散矩阵评分，无需额外度量且速度快 1.7–1.9 倍。引入主题间最大边际相关性步骤提升词汇多样性。

Result: MARETopic_Corr 在类别最多的两个基准上 Purity 和 NMI 领先，超越神经与聚类模型；MARETopic_Diff 推理加速 1.7–1.9 倍。无梯度更新条件下，在三个数据集中的两个上主题连贯性最优。词汇多样性显著提升且连贯性损失极小。

Conclusion: MARETopic 以无训练、基于秩的原型选择范式，解决了高维距离失真与潜在表示脱离文本的问题，实现了更高的主题质量、纯度、NMI 与效率，并直接输出真实文档作为可解释主题代表。

Abstract: Recent topic models leverage pretrained embeddings, but neural architectures produce latent representations without grounding in specific texts, and clustering-based pipelines assign representative documents only post hoc, relying on absolute distances distorted by hubness and anisotropy in high-dimensional spaces. We introduce MARETopic, a training-free framework that casts topic discovery as rank-based prototype selection. After projecting embeddings onto a low-dimensional manifold, MARETopic builds ranked lists encoding ordinal neighborhood structure. A greedy algorithm selects exactly K exemplar documents, real corpus texts, whose neighborhoods cover the corpus. Two variants share this criterion. MARETopic$_\text{Corr}$ scores candidates with a query performance predictor and a rank correlation measure, leading Purity and NMI on the two benchmarks with the most categories, ahead of both neural and clustering-based topic models. MARETopic$_\text{Diff}$ scores them with a rank-based diffusion matrix, needs neither measure, and runs 1.7 to 1.9 times faster. Without a single gradient update, MARETopic leads topic coherence on two of three datasets. A novel inter-topic Maximal Marginal Relevance step raises vocabulary diversity at little cost in coherence. Our code is available at https://github.com/thcastilho/maretopic.

</details>


### [72] [Nearest-neighbour baselines for fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra under different assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02249)
*Ling Min Serena Khoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种最近邻检索变体在MS/MS谱分子指纹预测中的表现，揭示了不同推理时信息假设对性能的影响，旨在建立更严格的基线以推动该领域的严格基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究显示最近邻检索在分子指纹预测中表现强劲，甚至匹配或超越深度学习模型，但'最近邻'包含多种假设推理时可用信息不同的变体，缺乏系统比较和严格基线。

Method: 系统比较多个最近邻变体，分析其在推理时假设可用信息差异（如谱库大小、相似性度量、检索策略等）对分子指纹预测性能的影响。

Result: 揭示了不同信息假设显著影响检索性能，建立了更严格的基线基准，为该领域进展提供了更严谨的衡量标准。

Conclusion: 最近邻检索家族内部的假设差异导致性能显著变化，建立更严格的基线对于推动分子指纹预测领域的严格基准测试和准确衡量进展至关重要。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that nearest-neighbour retrieval provides a strong baseline for molecular fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra, with several variants matching or outperforming current deep learning models (Khoo and Barzilay, 2026; Liu et al., 2026; Gupta et al., 2026). Importantly, "nearest neighbour" encompasses a family of retrieval methods that differ in the information assumed to be available at inference. In this report, we systematically compare several nearest-neighbour variants and show how these differing assumptions affect performance. Our goal is to establish stricter baselines that enable more rigorous benchmarking and better measure progress in this area.

</details>


### [73] [Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling for Diffusion Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02251)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS)，一种基于秩感知列表耦合的验证规则，用于推测性草稿树。RASS 按提议-目标均值位移对草稿候选进行排序，采样优化权重的秩以最小化所选提议与目标分布的全变差距离，并将选中候选与目标最大耦合，残差修正保证精确采样。在高斯混合目标、FFHQ 无条件生成、CIFAR-10 条件生成及 Stable Diffusion 3.5 潜在扩散上评测，以标准与推测采样目标模型评估次数之比为指标，RASS 在所有设置上均优于 D-GRS，CIFAR-10 上在相同计算预算下提升约 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测性采样通过并行验证廉价草稿状态加速扩散生成并保持目标分布。树状方法（如 D-GRS）比单链草稿更有效地利用并行计算预算，但 D-GRS 按生成顺序依次测试候选。实际上采样得到的候选在无需额外目标模型评估的情况下即蕴含信息量排序，利用这一排序可进一步提升效率。

Method: RASS 引入基于秩感知列表耦合的验证规则：1) 按提议分布与目标分布的均值位移对草稿候选排序；2) 用优化权重采样秩，最小化所选提议分布与目标分布的全变差距离；3) 将选中候选与目标进行最大耦合；4) 通过残差修正确保任意秩权重选择下的精确采样。

Result: 在高斯混合目标、FFHQ 无条件像素空间生成、CIFAR-10 条件生成以及使用 COCO2014 提示词的 Stable Diffusion 3.5 潜在扩散四个实验中，以标准采样与推测采样目标模型评估次数之比为度量，RASS 在所有评测设置上均超越 D-GRS，在 CIFAR-10 上于匹配计算预算下增益约 20%。

Conclusion: RASS 通过秩感知列表耦合有效利用草稿候选的内在排序信息，在保持精确采样的前提下，为扩散生成任务提供了可度量的加速优于现有树状推测采样方法 D-GRS。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion generation by verifying inexpensive draft states in parallel while preserving the target law. Recent tree-based methods allocate the parallel compute budget more effectively than single-chain drafts, as demonstrated by Diffusion Greedy Rejection Sampling (D-GRS). D-GRS generates $K$ conditionally independent candidates per node, and sequentially tests them in their generation order. Yet the sampled candidates admit an informative ranking without additional target-model evaluations. To exploit this, we introduce Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS), a verification rule for speculative draft trees based on rank-aware list coupling. RASS orders draft candidates along the proposal-target mean displacement and samples a rank with weights optimized to minimize total variation between the selected-proposal and target laws. Finally, the selected candidate is maximally coupled with the target, with residual correction ensuring exact sampling for any choice of rank weights. We evaluate RASS on a Gaussian-mixture target, unconditional pixel-space generation on FFHQ, conditional generation on CIFAR-10, and latent diffusion with Stable Diffusion 3.5 using COCO2014 prompts. Measured by the ratio of standard to speculative sampling's target-model evaluation counts, RASS improves on D-GRS across the evaluated settings, with gains reaching approximately 20% on CIFAR-10 at matched compute budgets.

</details>


### [74] [Counterfactual Predictions in Scientific Emulators Without Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02252)
*Dingling Yao,Kahaan Gandhi,Valentin Duruisseaux,Boris Bonev,Francesco Locatello,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReRoute 是一种用于科学反事实预测的框架，结合事实数据与部分机制知识，无需受控干预数据即可实现准确的 what-if 预测。在平流扩散系统和气候模拟任务中，ReRoute 显著降低了反事实预测误差（18.2-31.8%），且计算成本远低于重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究常需回答反事实问题（如条件、干预或历史不同会怎样）。现有模型在观测数据上表现良好，但在相关输入独立变化时失效。常见补救是引入受控模拟数据，但需要模拟器、计算昂贵且继承模拟器假设。因此需要一种无需受控干预数据、结合事实数据与部分机制知识的方法。

Method: ReRoute 固定预训练骨干网络的查询输入为参考值，通过已知机制路径重新引入其变化，在原始事实数据上微调，保留下游效应由学习到的动力学捕捉。提供了因果识别理论结果，并在 Lean 中机器验证核心论证。

Result: 在受控平流扩散系统中实现高精度反事实预测。在最先进的气候模拟器上，面对严重 CO2 分布偏移的耦合气候干预，聚合气候误差降低 18.2-31.8%，标准条件下技能保持不变，成本仅为重新训练的小部分。在基于 ERA5 再分析训练的模拟器上，固定 CO2 反事实下保留了更多观测边界条件暗示的地表变暖。

Conclusion: ReRoute 通过将部分机制知识注入预训练模型，实现了低成本、高精度的科学反事实预测，无需昂贵的受控模拟数据，为气候科学等领域的 what-if 推理提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Many scientific questions require reasoning about what was never observed: What if the conditions, interventions, or history had been different? Models can predict accurately on observed data yet fail on such what-if queries when correlated inputs are varied independently. A common remedy is to add controlled simulation data in which these factors are explicitly disentangled, but this requires access to a simulator, can be computationally expensive, and inherits the simulator's modeling assumptions. We introduce ReRoute, a framework for targeted scientific what-if prediction that combines factual data with partial mechanistic knowledge, without requiring controlled intervention data for adaptation. ReRoute fixes the queried input of a pretrained backbone to a reference value, reintroduces its variation through a known mechanistic pathway, and fine-tunes on the original factual data, while leaving downstream effects to the learned dynamics. We provide a causal identification result for this construction under explicit structural assumptions, with the core argument machine-checked in Lean. After showing that ReRoute achieves highly accurate counterfactual predictions in a controlled advection-diffusion system where exact responses are available, we turn to state-of-the-art climate emulation. On held-out coupled-climate interventions, ReRoute reduces aggregate climate error by 18.2-31.8% under severe CO$_2$ distribution shifts while preserving skill under standard conditions, at a small fraction of the cost of retraining on additional controlled simulations, without even accounting for the substantial expense of generating such data. Finally, on an emulator trained from historical ERA5 reanalysis, where no counterfactual reference exists, ReRoute preserves substantially more of the surface warming implied by the observed boundary conditions under a fixed-CO$_2$ counterfactual.

</details>


### [75] [Approximation Property of Dropout Neural Networks: Sobolev Rates and Confidence Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02253)
*Jia-He Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Dropout 神经网络的通用近似性质，给出了 ReLU 网络在边以概率 p 独立保留下逼近 Sobolev 空间单位球的上下界，上下界在精度指数上匹配，在置信度上也基本匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Dropout 神经网络在随机边保留下的近似能力，量化网络规模与近似精度、置信度的关系，填补通用近似性质未描述网络规模的空白。

Method: 构造常数深度的 ReLU 网络，结合有界局部子网络、成功近似事件上的局部化、多尺度 Taylor 分解；利用 Sobolev 容量和仿射函数近似推导下界。

Result: 上界网络规模为 �~ O_{n,d}(p^{-9} ε^{-max{d/n,2}} log(1/δ))，下界显示精度指数 max{d/n,2} 与输出层代价 ((1-p)/p) ε^{-2} log(1/δ) 是必需的；上下界在精度指数上匹配，在 d≤2n 时置信度上也匹配；扩展到 W^{n,r} 目标与 L^s 误差。

Conclusion: 对于固定保留概率 p 和高置信度，Dropout ReLU 网络的近似性能在精度指数上达到最优，最优保留概率依赖和对数因子仍待解决。

Abstract: The universal approximation property of dropout neural networks does not by itself describe the network size required for an accurate random realization. In this work, we study approximation of the unit ball of $W^{n,\infty}([0,1]^d)$ by ReLU networks whose edges are retained independently with probability $p$. The approximation error is measured uniformly over the input domain, and the guarantee holds with probability at least $1-δ$ for a single sampled network. We construct networks of constant depth and size $\widetilde O_{n,d}(p^{-9}\varepsilon^{-\max\{d/n,2\}} \log(1/δ))$. The construction combines bounded local subnetworks, localization on a successful approximation event, and a multiscale Taylor decomposition. Conversely, Sobolev capacity imposes a lower bound on the number of surviving edges, while approximation of a fixed affine function requires an output-layer cost of order $((1-p)/p)\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ)$ at sufficiently high confidence. For fixed $p\in(0,1)$ and $δ<\min\{1/2,1-p\}$, the upper and lower bounds match in the accuracy exponent under a fixed or logarithmic depth budget. When $d\leq2n$, they also match in confidence up to logarithms of accuracy. We extend the lower bounds to $W^{n,r}$ targets with $L^s$ error, and distinguish this extension from the upper bound for $W^{n,\infty}$. The optimal retention dependence and logarithmic factors remain open.

</details>


### [76] [Overcoming Challenges of Interpretive Structural Modeling with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02254)
*Everett Rush,David J. Icove,Ari Kim,Byung H. Park,Michael A. Langston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）与解释性结构建模（ISM）的集成方法，旨在解决传统ISM依赖专家反复交互、难以扩展到大规模变量的问题。通过对比成对、k-wise、行级和全图四种因果图发现方法，发现行级方法（SHD=160, F1=0.77）和全图方法（SHD=135, F1=0.73）在ISM因果图发现任务中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ISM建模过程需专家反复交互达成共识，耗时费力，且难以扩展到数百变量的研究。现有工作利用LLM作为不完美专家进行因果图发现，提供了自动化ISM的可能性。

Method: 提出集成LLM-ISM方法，系统对比了四种因果图发现策略：成对（pairwise）、k-wise、行级（rowwise）和全图（full graph）发现，并使用结构汉明距离（SHD）和F1-score评估性能。

Result: 行级方法达到SHD=160、F1-score=0.77；全图方法达到SHD=135、F1-score=0.73。两者均优于成对和k-wise方法，验证了LLM辅助ISM在大规模变量下的可行性。

Conclusion: LLM可作为不完美专家有效辅助ISM建模，行级和全图发现策略在因果关系识别上表现最优，为大规模多准则决策自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Interpretive Structural Modeling (ISM) is a well-known process for multi-criteria decision making. The success of ISM over other methodologies is its ability to model causal relationships, the binary scale of factors, and resulting hierarchical representation. Traditionally, the modeling process is performed by repeated interactions with subject matter experts until consensus is reached. This process is tedious, labor-intense, and most importantly limits the ability of ISM to scale to studies with hundreds of variables. Drawing on existing work of causal graph discovery with large language models (LLM) as imperfect experts, this work explores an integrated LLM-ISM approach for ISM. Pairwise, k-wise, rowwise, and full graph discovery methodologies are compared and evaluated. It is shown that causal graph discovery methods for ISM perform best using rowwise (SHD=160, F1-score=0.77) and full graph methods (SHD=135, F1-score=0.73).

</details>


### [77] [MACTS-EM: Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02255)
*Ahmad Shahi,Mamehgol Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多智能体协作时间序列预测框架（MACTS-EM），结合领域专用智能体、元认知层、涌现记忆机制、多模态上下文集成和对抗鲁棒性组件，在多个领域实验中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法难以处理复杂现象，如机制转移、跨域知识迁移和多模态数据融合，需要一种能够协作、适应上下文并具备多尺度时间理解的新范式。

Method: MACTS-EM架构包含五大核心组件：(1)领域专用预测智能体，分别负责模式识别、异常检测、因果推断和不确定性量化；(2)元认知层动态分配智能体；(3)涌现记忆机制实现跨域模式迁移；(4)多模态上下文集成；(5)对抗鲁棒性组件。

Result: 在金融市场、气候模式、能源消耗和疫情传播四个领域实验中，MACTS-EM相比现有方法预测准确率提升8-12%，零样本迁移能力提升22-27%，机制转移期间韧性增强16-21%，分布偏移后恢复速度加快15-18%。

Conclusion: 协作式智能体时间序列预测方法是超越传统架构的有前景方向，特别适合需要多尺度时间理解和上下文适应的复杂真实场景。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains a critical challenge across numerous domains. Despite significant advancements, existing approaches struggle with complex phenomena such as regime shifts, cross-domain knowledge transfer, and multimodal data integration. This paper introduces Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory (MACTS-EM), a novel framework where specialised agents collaborate to achieve superior forecasting performance. The MACTS-EM architecture integrates: (1) domain-specialised forecasting agents for pattern recognition, anomaly detection, causal inference, and uncertainty quantification; (2) a meta-cognitive layer for dynamic agent allocation; (3) an emergent memory mechanism enabling cross-domain pattern transfer; (4) multimodal contextual integration; and (5) adversarial robustness components. Evaluation across financial markets, climate patterns, energy consumption, and pandemic propagation demonstrates that MACTS-EM outperforms existing approaches in most scenarios, with 8-12% improvement in forecasting accuracy, 22-27% better zero-shot transfer capability, 16-21% enhanced resilience during regime shifts, and 15-18% faster recovery after distribution shifts.
