Our findings suggest that collaborative, agentic approaches to time series forecasting represent a promising direction beyond traditional architectures, particularly for complex real-world scenarios requiring multi-resolution temporal understanding and contextual adaptation.

</details>


### [78] [TRACE: A Reproducible Benchmark for Electricity Price Forecasting with Official Operational Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02256)
*Xinyi Yi,Moy Yuan,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE基准，首次系统评估电力价格预测中预测时刻文本上下文的价值，包含5个区域7300个区域-日实例，重建预测截止时刻的官方运行文本防止信息泄露，实验显示文本信息使上尾风险针球损失中位数降低7.4%，文本错配消融实验证实了增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力价格预测基准主要关注数值输入，预测时刻文本上下文（如官方运行文本）的预测价值评估不足，缺乏可复现的基准来量化文本对价格中枢波动及尾部风险的语义对齐与预测增益。

Method: 构建TRACE基准：从美国主要电力市场5个区域收集7300个区域-日实例，将实时价格与预测截止时刻可获得的官方运行文本配对；在每个预测截止点重建官方文本，严格防止截止后信息泄露；使用时间序列基础模型评估语义对齐度（对中枢、上下尾部风险）与预测价值（针球损失），并设计跨日文本错配消融实验验证因果性。

Result: 语义评估显示文本与真实价格的中枢波动及上下尾部风险均对齐，上尾价格风险对齐最稳健；预测价值体现为跨基础模型上尾针球损失中位数下降7.4%；跨日文本错配消融实验导致增益消失，表现甚至劣于无文本基线，确认了文本信息的因果贡献。

Conclusion: TRACE填补了电力价格预测中文本上下文评估的空白，证明预测时刻官方运行文本对上尾价格风险预测有显著增益，强调防止信息泄露的关键性，为文本增强型电力价格预测提供了可复现的评估标准。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting (EPF) supports scheduling, bidding, and risk management in electricity markets, yet existing benchmarks focus mainly on numerical inputs, leaving the forecasting value of forecast-time textual context insufficiently evaluated. We introduce TRACE, a reproducible benchmark of 7,300 zone--day instances pairing prices from five zones in a major U.S. market with official operational text available at the forecast cutoff. TRACE reconstructs official operational text at each cutoff, preventing post-cutoff information leakage. We evaluate TRACE for semantic alignment and forecasting value. Semantic assessments align with central movement and both tail risks in ground-truth prices, most consistently for upper-tail price risk. Forecasting value is reflected in a median 7.4\% reduction in upper-tail pinball loss across time-series foundation models. A controlled cross-day text-mismatch ablation reverses the gains, falling below the no-text baseline.

</details>


### [79] [Parameter-Free Interval-Dynamic Regret under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02258)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在线凸优化问题，设定为每轮获得一个无偏随机次梯度，且噪声具有未知的有限条件 p 阶矩 (1<p≤2)。提出了一种自适应学习器，无需知道梯度界 G、噪声尺度 σ、噪声指数 p、区间 I 或比较器路径变化量 P_I，即可在任意固定区间 I 上达到近似最优的期望动态遗憾界。该界同时捕捉均值梯度项和噪声项的不同指数，且常数因子是通用的。理论分析包含区间自适应、专家分布比较、先验设计、下界证明等多个方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线凸优化算法通常需要知道梯度界 G、噪声方差 σ² 或噪声指数 p 等超参数，且多关注静态遗憾或固定分区的动态遗憾。论文旨在设计一个完全无参数、自适应未知噪声矩条件且能在任意区间上竞争任意比较器路径的学习器，同时保持均值梯度项和噪声项的最优指数分离，并给出匹配的下界。

Method: 核心方法是基于专家加权框架，构建一系列在不同时间尺度（窗口长度）和重启长度上运行的基础学习器，利用非均匀先验分布（倾向于长窗口和长重启）来实现区间自适应。分析通过控制期望校准误差和观测尺度变化的代价，推导出通用的遗憾界。同时采用度量变换技术证明下界，确立对数因子的必要性。

Result: 对于任意长度为 n 的区间 I 和比较器路径复杂度 Λ_I = 1 + P_I/D，学习器达到的期望遗憾界为：E[Regret_I(u)] ≤ min(GD n, C[GD √(n(Λ_I + log²(2T))) + σ D n^{1/p} (Λ_I + log²(2T))^{(p-1)/p}])。该界同时自适应区间 I、比较器路径变化 P_I、噪声参数 σ 和 p，且常数 C 是通用的。区间代价为 1 + log(T/n)，包含最优全时域静态率。下界证明了保持全时域最优保证的学习器必须支付该对数因子。

Conclusion: 论文建立了在未知有限 p 阶噪声矩条件下，完全自适应的在线凸优化理论。提出的学习器无需任何先验知识，在任意区间上对任意比较器路径达到近似最优的动态遗憾，且均值梯度项和噪声项的指数分离是本质最优的。非均匀先验设计使得区间自适应代价仅为对数级。下界结果确认了对数因子的不可避免性。相同的决策框架也自然推广到静态比较和确定性分区设置。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization with one unbiased stochastic subgradient per round and an unknown finite conditional $p$th noise moment, $1<p\le2$. For every fixed interval $I$ of length $n$ and comparator path with $Λ_I=1+P_I/D$, one learner achieves
  \[ E[Regret_I(u)]\le\min(GDn, C[GD\sqrt{n(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))} +σDn^{1/p}(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))^{(p-1)/p}]). \]
  The learner uses none of $G,σ,p,I,P_I$, and the constant is universal. Interval adaptation adds to comparator complexity, preserving the distinct mean-gradient and noise exponents. The analysis controls calibration in expectation and limits the cost of observation-scale changes. Its general theorem compares to distributions over predictably available experts with relative-entropy dependence on a nonuniform prior. A common prior favors long windows and long restart lengths. With the statistics supplied, the interval cost becomes $1+\log(T/n)$, including the optimal full-horizon static rate. A change-of-measure lower bound identifies the noise power of this logarithm for learners retaining a full-horizon optimal guarantee, under explicit conditions. Static comparisons and deterministic partitions follow from the same decisions.

</details>


### [80] [From Mathematical to Executable Certificates for Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02268)
*Ziyu Zhao,Xinyu Wang,Xiaowen Chang,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ExecCert（可执行发布认证），一种发布时认证层，用于弥合认证机器遗忘的数学保证与实际部署中有限精度制品之间的差距。ExecCert通过闭合原生证书或应用重训参考发布验证（RRV）来认证候选制品。针对冻结表示配可变岭回归头的场景，开发了增量RRV实现，在序列删除请求中维护认证证据而非每次重建。实验显示ExecCert能保留有效证书、修正发布决策、收紧保守界限，且增量认证在发布检查足够频繁时比替代方案更廉价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘在删除请求、过时记录或数据质量问题时需要移除数据，但从头重训成本高昂。认证机器遗忘方法提供数学保证，但实际部署系统发布的是软件产生的具体有限精度制品。数学保证与实际部署制品之间存在差距，需要一种发布时认证机制来验证候选制品是否满足认证要求。

Method: 提出ExecCert作为发布时认证层：1）尝试为执行的候选制品闭合方法的原生证书；2）若失败则应用重训参考发布验证（RRV），认证制品对当前保留集重训的保真度。针对序列删除导致的精确保留集参考与存储数值状态分别演化的问题，针对冻结表示配可变岭回归头的场景，开发了增量RRV实现，跨删除请求维护认证证据而非每次重建。

Result: 在四个已发布的遗忘实现上，ExecCert保留了有效证书、改变了发布决策、收紧了保守界限，并识别了具体输出支持的重训参考保真度。在序列服务实验中，RRV消除了存储方程验证导致的错误发布，同时紧密跟踪实际误差；增量认证在发布检查足够频繁时比全新验证因子和维护验证因子替代方案都更廉价。

Conclusion: ExecCert成功弥合了认证机器遗忘的理论保证与实际部署制品之间的差距，通过发布时认证和增量RRV机制，提供了实用且高效的认证方案，能够处理序列删除场景下的认证维护问题，在保证正确性的同时降低了认证成本。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is needed when data must be removed because of deletion requests, outdated records, or data-quality concerns, while retraining from scratch can be costly. Certified machine unlearning methods provide mathematical guarantees, while deployed systems release concrete finite-precision artifacts produced by software. To bridge the gap between mathematical guarantees and practical deployment, we introduce Executable Release Certification (ExecCert), a release-time layer that certifies the candidate artifact considered for release. ExecCert either closes a method's native certificate for the executed candidate or applies Retraining-Reference Release Verification (RRV) to certify fidelity to current retain-set retraining. Sequential deletion makes the latter nontrivial because the exact retain-set reference and the stored numerical state evolve separately. For frozen representations with a mutable ridge head, we develop an incremental realization of RRV that maintains certified evidence across deletion requests rather than reconstructing it at each release. On four published unlearning implementations, ExecCert preserves valid certificates, changes release decisions, tightens conservative bounds, and identifies the retraining-reference fidelity supported by concrete outputs. In sequential-service experiments, RRV eliminates false releases caused by stored-equation verification while closely tracking realized error, and incremental certification remains cheaper than both fresh and maintained verified-factor alternatives once release checks become sufficiently frequent.

</details>


### [81] [CLEAN: Psychometrically Consistent Incremental Cognitive Diagnosis under Concept-Space Expansion via Architectural Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02278)
*Tao He,Jinxing Xiang,Fan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAN框架，通过架构隔离实现认知诊断中的增量概念空间扩展，保证历史诊断结果的逐点不变性，实现零表示漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量认知诊断模型假设概念空间固定，新增项目导致灾难性遗忘，且软约束无法保证心理测量一致性（诊断不变性），需要结构化保证历史诊断不变的增量学习方法。

Method: CLEAN采用严格拓扑二分协议冻结历史诊断函数，利用确定性正交列掩码切断梯度干扰；部署可扩展全秩分支并以微方差初始化学习新概念；形式化表示漂移（RD）量化历史特征扰动，从架构层面保证不变性。

Result: 在三个大规模教育数据集上，CLEAN实现零RD，旧项目指标与静态锚点完全一致，新项目性能与强基线持平或更优。

Conclusion: CLEAN通过架构隔离而非软约束，实现了认知诊断增量学习中的心理测量一致性，为动态概念空间扩展提供了理论与实践保证。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis (CD) is a fundamental task in intelligent education that profiles learner proficiency over knowledge concepts. In real-world learning platforms, newly added items continually introduce previously unseen concepts, necessitating dynamic expansion of the underlying concept space. Yet existing incremental CD models assume a fixed concept space, allowing gradients from new items to overwrite historical pathways and induce catastrophic forgetting. More critically, these methods rely solely on soft constraints to preserve historical diagnoses. Such constraints may fail to satisfy the requirement of diagnostic invariance after incremental updates, a requirement known as psychometric consistency in cognitive diagnosis. Therefore, we propose CLEAN (Continual Learning with Expandable and Architecturally Isolated Networks), a novel incremental CD framework supporting concept-space expansion while providing structural guarantees for pointwise invariance of historical diagnoses. Specifically, CLEAN first introduces a strict topological bipartition protocol, freezes historical diagnostic functions and applies deterministic orthogonal column masking to sever gradient interference. Second, to accommodate concept expansion, expandable full-rank branches with micro-variance initialization are deployed to learn novel concepts. Finally, to verify that this architectural design achieves invariance by construction, we formalize Representation Drift (RD) to quantify the perturbation of historical traits. Extensive experiments on three large-scale educational datasets demonstrate that CLEAN achieves zero RD, preserving old-item metrics identically to static anchors through architectural isolation while remaining competitive with or superior to strong continual-learning baselines on new items.

</details>


### [82] [MuLoRA: Spectrally Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02283)
*Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了LoRA在持续学习中存在的“谱可塑性坍塌”问题：更新能量集中在少数奇异模式中，导致低秩空间利用不足。提出MuLoRA方法，通过历史白化和极分解正交化联合控制容量分配与利用，在5个类增量基准和8个增量设置中，16个指标中15个达到最高平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的名义秩掩盖了有效适应能力的损失。在顺序适应过程中，更新能量集中在少数奇异模式，大部分低秩空间未被利用。仅靠干扰避免（保护历史表征）不足以保证剩余适应容量对新任务的响应性和有效利用。

Method: 提出MuLoRA，包含两大机制：1) 历史白化：识别相对于累积历史响应具有强当前任务响应的输入方向，产生训练期间固定的任务自适应基；2) 动量更新的近似极正交化：在选定空间内降低谱集中度。正交归一化基连接两机制，精确传递因子更新谱到诱导权重更新。建立精确子空间选择的极大极小刻画，并在受控跨步各向异性下推导累积谱界。

Result: 在5个类增量基准和8个增量设置的实验中，MuLoRA在16个报告指标中有15个达到最高平均准确率。

Conclusion: MuLoRA通过联合控制容量分配（历史白化）和利用（极正交化），有效解决了LoRA持续学习中的谱可塑性坍塌问题，显著提升了低秩适应空间的利用效率和新任务学习性能。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) provides a parameter-efficient approach to continual learning, but its nominal rank can conceal a loss of effective adaptation capacity. We identify \emph{spectral plasticity collapse}: during sequential adaptation, update energy becomes concentrated in a small subset of singular modes, leaving much of the available low-rank space underutilized. This exposes a limitation of interference avoidance alone: protecting historical representations does not ensure that the remaining adaptation capacity is responsive to new tasks or effectively utilized. To address this problem, we propose \texttt{MuLoRA}, which jointly controls capacity allocation and utilization. First, historical whitening identifies input directions with strong current-task response relative to accumulated historical response, yielding a task-adaptive basis that remains fixed during training. Second, approximate polar orthogonalization of momentum updates reduces spectral concentration within theselected space. An orthonormal basis connects these mechanisms by transferring the factor-update spectrum exactly tothe induced weight update. We establish a max--min characterization of exact subspace selection and derive cumulative spectral bounds under controlled cross-step anisotropy. Across five class-incremental benchmarks and eight incremental settings, \texttt{MuLoRA} achieves the highest mean accuracy in 15 of 16 reported metrics.

</details>


### [83] [Effects of interpulse-interval variation on deep-learning classification of bat vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02284)
*Welmoed R. Eversteijn,Burooj Ghani,A. Leonie Baier,Dan Stowell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探究脉冲间间隔（IPI）变化对蝙蝠物种分类的贡献，以及Transformer模型（PaSST）是否比CNN（EfficientNet-B0）更有效地利用该时序信息。实验表明：自然IPI变化对分类性能的提升有限，Transformer并未显著优于CNN；但跨条件评估显示，在标准化IPI上训练的模型在自然IPI测试集上性能下降，提示标准化条件下的结果可能无法完全迁移到自然录音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序上下文可能有助于自动蝙蝠物种分类，但具体特征（如IPI）的贡献尚不清晰。研究旨在量化IPI变化提供的物种判别信息，并对比Transformer架构与CNN对该信息的敏感度差异。

Method: 构建两个匹配数据集：自然IPI条件（保留原始呼叫时序）和标准化IPI条件（呼叫起始点间隔固定为50ms）。分别在两种条件下微调EfficientNet-B0和PaSST，并进行条件内评估。额外开展跨条件实验：分别在自然IPI和标准化IPI训练集上训练各架构，并在同一自然IPI测试集上评估。同时评估预训练分类器BatDetect2和BAT在两种条件下的表现。

Result: 条件内评估：PaSST在自然IPI（71±2.3%）和标准化IPI（70±6.3%）下准确率相近；EfficientNet从自然IPI（47±4.7%）显著提升至标准化IPI（57±3.9%）。PaSST在两条件下均优于EfficientNet。跨条件评估：自然IPI训练的模型在自然IPI测试集上优于标准化IPI训练的模型——EfficientNet从54%降至50%，PaSST从65%降至57%。预训练分类器BatDetect2和BAT在两种IPI条件下差异极小。

Conclusion: 总体发现对自然IPI变化显著贡献于蝙蝠物种分类、且Transformer比CNN更有效利用该信息的假设支持有限。然而，跨条件性能下降表明，在标准化IPI条件下获得的结果可能无法完全泛化到自然录音场景。

Abstract: Temporal context may aid automated bat-species classification, but the contribution of specific features remains unclear. We investigated whether variation in the interpulse interval (IPI)-the time between consecutive call onsets-provides species-discriminative information and whether transformer-based models are more sensitive to this information than convolutional neural networks. We created two matched datasets from European bat recordings: a natural-IPI condition retaining the original call timing and a normalized-IPI condition in which call onsets were spaced at 50-ms intervals. EfficientNet-B0 and PaSST were fine-tuned and evaluated within each condition. In an additional experiment, each architecture was trained separately on natural-IPI and normalized-IPI recordings, and evaluated on the same natural-IPI test set. Finally, the pretrained classifiers BatDetect2 and BAT were evaluated on both conditions. Within-condition IPI normalization had model-dependent effects. PaSST accuracy differed little between the natural-IPI ($71 \pm 2.3\%$) and normalized-IPI ($70 \pm 6.3\%$) conditions, whereas EfficientNet accuracy increased from $47 \pm 4.7\%$ to $57 \pm 3.9\%$. PaSST exceeded EfficientNet under both conditions. In the cross-condition evaluation, models trained on natural-IPI recordings outperformed those trained on normalized-IPI recordings on the natural-IPI test set: accuracy decreased from 54% to 50% for EfficientNet and from 65% to 57% for PaSST. BatDetect2 and BAT differed little between IPI conditions. Overall, we found limited support for the hypotheses that natural IPI variation contributes substantially to bat-species classification and that it is used more effectively by transformer-based than CNN-based models. Nevertheless, the cross-condition performance decrease shows that results obtained under normalized conditions may not transfer fully to natural recordings.

</details>


### [84] [Diffusion-Based Synthetic Data Pretraining for Enhancing Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02292)
*E. Riveros,D. Vega-Oliveros,A. Soriano-Vargas,A. Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型生成合成传感器数据的两阶段训练策略：先在合成数据上预训练CABiGRU模型，再在真实DEO数据集上微调，实现进食/饮水活动识别的90.6%平衡准确率，显著优于监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAR系统在细微且代表性不足的饮食活动类别（进食/饮水）上表现不佳，类别不平衡限制了其在真实饮食监测中的实用性。

Method: 采用CABiGRU架构（卷积层+双向GRU+多头注意力+残差连接）处理智能手表加速度计/陀螺仪/磁力计数据；利用扩散模型生成合成传感器窗口；实施两阶段训练：合成数据预训练→真实数据微调。

Result: 在DEO数据集上达到90.6%平衡准确率，优于强监督基线，验证了扩散模型合成预训练对识别饮食活动和处理类别不平衡的有效性。

Conclusion: 扩散生成数据与针对性微调的结合增强了饮食行为识别的鲁棒性，为医疗保健和营养监测场景的可靠部署迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is increasingly important for healthcare, well-being, and daily monitoring ap- plications, for which detecting alimentary activities such as eating and drinking can provide actionable insight into dietary habits and chronic disease management. HAR systems, however, often underperform on subtle and underrepresented classes, limiting their utility in real-world dietary monitoring. This work builds upon CABiGRU, a convolutional architecture with Bidirectional GRU layers, multi-head attention, and residual connections, designed to capture discriminative temporal patterns from smart- watch accelerometer, gyroscope, and magnetometer data. To improve CaBiGRU's generalization and reduce underfitting in the minority class, we leverage synthetic sensor data windows using a diffusion model and adopt a two-stage training strategy: pre-training CABiGRU on synthetic data, followed by fine-tuning on the real-world data. On the DEO (drinking/eating/other) dataset, the proposed pipeline achieves a balanced accuracy of 90.6%, improving over a strong supervised baseline and showing the benefits of diffusion-based synthetic pre-training for recognizing alimentary activities and representing a step forward dealing with unbalanced classes. These results suggest that combining diffusion-generated data with targeted fine-tuning enhances robust recognition of dietary behaviors, supporting more reliable deployment in healthcare and nutrition-monitoring settings.

</details>


### [85] [$Ψ$-Resilience: Model-Free Feature Importance from 1D Topological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02299)
*Fabian Galis,Darian Onchis,Pedro Real Jurado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ψ-Resilience，一种无需模型的特征重要性方法，基于一维拓扑信号从数据直接导出解释，具有可审计性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征重要性方法通常依赖预测模型，缺乏分布层面的可审计性和鲁棒性；需要一种无模型、基于数据分布、可通过拓扑持久性量化鲁棒性的解释方法。

Method: 估计类条件密度，沿特征轴计算点wise绝对差构建类别分歧景观；利用该一维信号的0维持久同调定义韧性泛函，聚合在用户设定的鲁棒性尺度下存活的拓扑特征，得到上下文鲁棒的重要性分数。

Result: 在合成数据上Spearman秩相关高达0.8，与SHAP和互信息等方法竞争；在真实数据上与其他方法相关性高达0.9，展示稳定性和一致性。

Conclusion: Ψ-Resilience提供了一种稳定、可审计的无模型特征重要性方法，支持分布层面的严格审计，不依赖预测模型。

Abstract: We introduce $Ψ$-Resilience, a model-free feature importance method that derives explanations directly from the data itself via 1D topological signals. Our method constructs a class-disagreement landscape by estimating class-conditional densities and taking their pointwise absolute difference along the feature axis. Then, the 0-dimensional persistence of this 1D signal defines a resilience functional that aggregates only those topological features that survive perturbations up to a robustness scale which is set by the user. This gives us a context-robust importance score that is inherently auditable via the underlying 1D landscapes and their persistence. We evaluate our method on both synthetic and real datasets. On synthetic generators with specified ground-truth importance, $Ψ$-Resilience recovers the ranking of features with high fidelity, achieving Spearman rank correlations up to 0.8 and performing competitively with multiple feature importance methods, including SHAP and mutual information. On real datasets with no known ground truth, our technique agrees with these methods, with correlations up to 0.9. These results show that $Ψ$-Resilience is a stable explanation method that enables rigorous, distribution-level auditing of feature importance without relying on a predictive model.

</details>


### [86] [PowerBench: Measuring Language Model Bias in Power-shifting Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02303)
*Nicolas Martorell,Wendy Brau,Gonzalo A. Heredia,Tomás Pablo Korenblit,Gaspar Labastié,Tomás Gimenez Molina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PowerBench 评估框架，系统测试语言模型在权力转移请求上的拒答行为，发现模型对权力攫取拒答率最高，其次是剥夺权力，最后是自我赋能；模型存在国家偏见，倾向于帮助他人从美国夺权、反对美国用户从他人夺权，但支持美国在无损他人情况下获权；AI 代理作为用户时拒答率上升；语言偏见因模型而异但平均抵消。开源 PowerBench 以量化这些不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地协助涉及权力的请求，若模型系统性地偏向帮助特定群体，可能导致权力分布在大规模上发生偏移，或被用户利用身份差异规避拒答。因此需要系统评估模型在权力转移请求上的行为不对称性。

Method: 构建并开源 PowerBench 数据集，包含自我赋能、剥夺权力、权力攫取三类权力转移请求及不转移权力的拒答诱导控制请求。变量涵盖权力领域、语境、受影响方规模、用户先前权力地位。在三种实验条件下评估 24 个模型（12 个美国、12 个中国开发）：用户与受影响方国籍互换、AI 代理作为用户、8 种请求语言。

Result: 模型拒答率：权力攫取 > 剥夺权力 > 自我赋能。权力攫取拒答率随受影响方规模从个体到社会上升。模型偏向帮助他人从美国夺权、反对美国用户从他人夺权，但支持美国在无损他人情况下获权。AI 代理作为用户时拒答率显著上升，尤其是针对个体的权力攫取。语言导致拒答偏见，但呈模型特异性，平均层面大多抵消。

Conclusion: 发布 PowerBench 基准，使当前及未来模型中的权力转移不对称性可被量化测量，为理解和缓解语言模型在权力相关交互中的偏见提供工具。

Abstract: Language models increasingly assist people with power-related requests, so systematic differences in whom they help could shift the distribution of power at scale, or be exploited by users who learn which identities are refused less. We introduce PowerBench, an evaluation of power-shifting requests that distinguishes self-empowerment, disempowerment, and power grabbing, plus a control of refusal-inducing requests that shift no power. We build, curate, and open-source a dataset of such requests varying the power domain, the context, the scale of the affected party, and the prior power standing of the user, and evaluate 24 models (12 from US and 12 from Chinese developers) under three experimental conditions: reciprocal nationalities of user and affected party, an AI agent as the user, and 8 request languages. Models refuse power grabbing more than disempowerment, and disempowerment more than self-empowerment. Refusal of power grabbing rises with the scale of the affected party, from an individual to a society. Models are biased toward helping others take power from the US and against helping US users take power from others, but favor the US when it gains power and nobody loses it. When the user is an AI agent, refusal of power-shifting requests increases, especially in power grabbing against an individual. Finally, language biases refusal, but in model-specific ways that largely cancel on average. We release PowerBench to make these asymmetries measurable in current and future models.

</details>


### [87] [Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02324)
*Xiaofei Yin,Tong Chu,Jiyuan Fu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (SF-MOPD)，通过快慢双模型机制缓解多教师蒸馏中的能力干扰，兼顾通用能力保持与领域专长获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏(MOPD)虽能整合领域专长，但会导致学生模型逐渐偏离初始化模型，造成通用能力下降（能力干扰）；直接约束学生向初始化靠拢又会抑制领域专长习得。

Method: SF-MOPD引入快模型（当前学生，直接接受各教师更新）与慢模型（学生的指数移动平均）。慢模型作为动态能力参考，融合通用基础与已确立领域专长。对每个教师，在对数概率空间计算教师诱导的更新，仅移除将快模型推离慢模型的分量，保留对齐与正交分量。

Result: 多规模实验表明，SF-MOPD有效缓解能力干扰，增强专业多模态能力，降低通用能力基准上的平均退化，持续优于原版MOPD。

Conclusion: SF-MOPD通过快慢模型解耦与分量级更新筛选，实现了通用能力保持与领域专长获取的平衡，为多模态大模型的多教师蒸馏提供了更优范式。

Abstract: Foundation multimodal large language models are designed to support a broad spectrum of capabilities across diverse domains. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) provides an effective framework for consolidating domain-specific expertise into a single student model. However, MOPD training gradually drives the student away from its initialization model, and general capabilities decline as the displacement grows, resulting in capability interference. A direct remedy is constraining the student toward its initialization, but this suppresses the acquisition of domain expertise as well. We propose Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (SF-MOPD), which couples a fast model, the current student updated directly by each teacher, with a slow model, an exponential moving average of the student. The slow model absorbs the learning signal gradually, serving as a moving capability reference that fuses the general foundation with confirmed domain expertise. For each teacher, SF-MOPD computes the teacher-induced update in log-probability space and removes only the component that pushes the fast model further away from the slow model, while retaining aligned and orthogonal components. Experiments across multiple model scales demonstrate that SF-MOPD effectively mitigates capability interference, enhances specialized multimodal capabilities, and reduces the average degradation on general-capability benchmarks, consistently outperforming vanilla MOPD.

</details>


### [88] [Drive vs. Decay: On the Training Dynamics of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02344)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Seong Woo Ahn,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对JEPA模型中的表征崩塌问题，发展了一套早期训练稳定性理论，将经验性启发式方法统一在梯度流动力学框架下，提出了驱动力与衰减效应的竞争机制，并据此设计了ResidualPred预测器，显著提升了表征秩与下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA等联合嵌入预测架构在训练早期容易遭遇表征崩塌，现有的缓解手段主要依赖经验性启发式方法（如预测器缩放、掩码比例、EMA等），缺乏统一的理论解释。作者旨在建立一个能够统一这些启发式方法并指导设计新型稳定化组件的动力学理论。

Method: 在平凡不动点附近线性化耦合JEPA梯度流，识别出驱动力(γ)与衰减效应(σ)两个竞争项；在近似谱解耦假设下，逐模式稳定性比μ_i = γ_i/σ_i 分解为数据侧与预测器侧的独立项，不稳定模式数量决定可涌现表征的秩。理论预测相边界，并在800+ Tabular-JEPA配置上实验验证；将预测器缩放、掩码比例、EMA统一为调控μ的不同机制。基于此，提出ResidualPred——一种初始化时注意力偏向恒等映射的Transformer预测器。

Result: 理论框架准确预测了相边界，实验覆盖800+配置验证；ResidualPred在表格基准与I-JEPA预训练（CIFAR-10/100、STL-10、ImageNet）上均提升了有效秩与下游准确率，证明了理论驱动的稳定化设计优于经验启发式方法。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了JEPA早期训练动力学的统一理论图景，将表征崩塌的规避启发式方法与显式的梯度流稳定性分析联系起来，为设计理论指导的稳定化组件（如ResidualPred）提供了原则性依据，推动了自监督学习中稳定训练的理论与实践结合。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are prone to representation collapse, typically mitigated through empirical heuristics. We develop an early-training stability theory that unifies these heuristics. Linearising the coupled JEPA gradient flow around the trivial fixed point reveals two competing effects: a driving force ($γ$) and a decay effect ($σ$). Under approximate spectral decoupling, a per-mode stability ratio $μ_i = γ_i / σ_i$ factorises into independent data-side and predictor-side terms and the count of unstable modes tracks the rank of representations that can emerge. The framework predicts a phase boundary, which we confirm empirically across more than 800 Tabular-JEPA configurations. It also unifies predictor scaling, masking ratio, and EMA as distinct mechanisms for shifting $μ$. Guided by this analysis, we introduce ResidualPred, a transformer predictor whose attention is biased toward the identity at initialisation; it improves both effective rank and downstream accuracy on tabular benchmarks and in I-JEPA pretraining on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, STL-10, and ImageNet. Our framework connects empirical collapse-avoidance heuristics to an explicit dynamical picture, yielding theory-driven stabilizers. Code is available at https://github.com/jose-melo/drive-vs-decay.

</details>


### [89] [Mitigating Convergence Collapse in Fixed-Target Anomaly Detectors via Kernel-Anchored Locality Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02345)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了固定目标神经异常检测器（如收缩匹配、单步修正流、重构自编码器）存在“收敛崩溃”现象：优化越充分，检测性能反而越差。原因在于完全收敛时，学习到的映射会在分布外也跟踪目标，导致残差信号在异常和正常数据上同时消失。作者将核回归视角引入，提出了闭式免训练的核收缩匹配（KCM）作为基线，并设计了核锚定正则化器（KAR）惩罚神经网络预测偏离核加权训练目标平均，成功缓解崩溃并提升长训练下的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定目标神经异常检测器在结构上缺乏显式的局部性约束，依赖隐式的非收敛（早停、容量限制）来保留异常信号，这在理论上是不稳健的。经典检测器（kNN、KDE、孤立森林、LOF）显式强制局部性，而神经检测器允许无约束外推，导致收敛时残差信号在异常样本上也消失。需要从理论上形式化这种联系，并设计显式局部性约束来修复该结构性缺陷。

Method: 1. 理论表征收敛崩溃：证明完全收敛时映射在分布外也跟踪目标，残差信号全局消失。2. 建立核回归类比：证明固定目标检测器的核回归对应物是有限带宽的Nadaraya-Watson平滑器，称为核收缩匹配（KCM），具有闭式解、免训练、CPU高效特性。3. 提出核锚定正则化器（KAR）：在神经网络损失中加入正则项，惩罚预测值偏离训练目标的核加权局部平均，显式引入局部性约束。

Result: KCM作为免训练基线，在ADBench上性能媲美成熟的神经网络基线。KAR应用于三种主干网络（收缩匹配、单步修正流、重构自编码器），在易崩溃的ADBench数据集上显著缓解收敛崩溃，延长训练时持续提升AUROC，验证了显式局部性约束的有效性。

Conclusion: 有效的异常检测本质上需要显式的局部性约束以阻断无约束外推。固定目标神经检测器的结构性缺陷在于缺乏此类约束。KCM提供了强基线并揭示了核视角的理论本质；KAR通过将神经预测锚定到核加权局部平均，以最小代价修复了该缺陷，实现了收敛稳健的异常检测。

Abstract: A family of tabular anomaly detectors trains a neural map toward a fixed target under squared-error loss and scores anomalies by the test-time residual; contraction matching, one-step rectified flow, and reconstruction autoencoders all fit this template. We characterize a convergence collapse: better optimization makes the detector worse. At convergence, the learned map tracks the target even off-distribution, so the residual signal vanishes on anomalies as well as on normal data. These detectors therefore rely on implicit non-convergence (early stopping, capacity caps) to retain signal. We argue this is structural: effective anomaly detection requires a locality constraint that blocks unconstrained extrapolation. Classical detectors (kNN, KDE, isolation forests, LOF) enforce locality explicitly; fixed-target neural detectors do not. We formalize the connection by showing that the kernel-regression analog of a fixed-target detector is a finite-bandwidth Nadaraya-Watson smoother, which we call Kernel Contraction Matching (KCM). KCM is closed-form, training-free, and CPU-efficient, yet matches established neural baselines on ADBench. Building on this bridge, we introduce the Kernel-Anchored Regularizer (KAR), which penalizes deviation of the neural prediction from a kernel-weighted average of training targets. Across collapse-prone ADBench datasets and three backbones, KAR mitigates collapse and improves AUROC under prolonged training.

</details>


### [90] [From Behavior to Provenance: Attributing Tabular Foundation Models to Synthetic Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02347)
*Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Shivam Dubey,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Training-data attribution aims to identify which training examples shape model behavior, yet validating such claims is difficult because causal training influence is rarely observable. We argue that controlled synthetic pretraining makes attribution experimentally testable. Using O'PRIOR, a provenance-rich synthetic task generator for tabular foundation models, we construct a testbed in which every pretraining task carries explicit lineage over structural mechanisms, missingness, confounding, shortcuts, and distribution shift. We combine behavior-conditioned attribution with counterfactual retraining and provenance-aware interventions to test both task-level faithfulness and mechanism-level consistency. On held-out real tasks, removing the top-attributed 5% of synthetic tasks decreases mean ROC-AUC by 0.013, compared with 0.002$\pm$0.004 under random removal, while removing bottom-attributed tasks improves performance by 0.003. Within shortcut-provenance tasks, targeted removal yields an effect of 0.043 versus 0.016 for matched random removal. Provenance discrimination is more modest by ranking AUROC (0.55-0.62), despite substantial top-k enrichment, revealing that provenance association and interventional faithfulness need not coincide. Our results establish synthetic provenance as a controlled setting for verifiable contributive attribution

</details>


### [91] [Does Every User Need a Private LoRA? Decoupling Personalization from Per-User Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02353)
*Songyuan Sui,Srikanth Malla,Chiho Choi,Joon Hee Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LINEUP，一种基于共享低秩个性化因子库的个性化大语言模型方法，通过用户条件召回和查询依赖校准组合共享因子，仅需优化每用户8个标量即可实现高效个性化，大幅减少参数量并在多任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化大语言模型需为每个用户维护完整适配状态，随用户数增长扩展性差。本文从个性化容量分配视角重新审视：多少容量可跨用户共享、共享容量如何组成、多少必须用户专属。

Method: LINEUP学习可复用低秩个性化因子库，通过用户条件召回和查询依赖校准组合因子，将目标用户适配限制在共享校正空间上的极小用户编码（仅8个标量）。共享组件固定，仅优化用户编码。

Result: 在六个个性化任务（分类、预测、生成）的12个指标上均取得最佳性能（如LaMP-3 RMSE降低11.4%），在有限历史下仍保持优势。理论分析给出有限步有限历史风险界及用户编码优化优于历史初始化的充分条件。

Conclusion: 丰富个性化主要可由可复用、条件组合的共享容量支撑，独立用户适配仅需极小校正状态。该设计解耦了表达性个性化容量与每用户可训练状态，实现参数高效的大规模个性化。

Abstract: Personalized large language models often require a complete adaptation state for each user. However, this paradigm scales poorly as the user population grows. We revisit this design through the lens of personalization capacity allocation: how much adaptation capacity can be shared across users, how the shared capacity should be composed, and how much must remain user-specific. We answer them through three complementary empirical analyses. We find that independent user adapters contain substantial cross-user reusable structure, that the utility of reusable directions reflects both user relevance and variation across queries, and that user histories provide transferable signals for compact individual correction. Motivated by these findings, we propose LINEUP. It learns a bank of reusable low-rank personalization factors, composes them through user-conditioned recall and query-dependent calibration, and restricts target-user adaptation to a tiny user code over a shared correction space. This design decouples expressive personalization capacity from per-user trainable state. Each target user optimizes only eight scalars, while all shared components remain fixed. By comparison, the evaluated private-LoRA configuration uses 4.19 million per-user parameters. Our theoretical analysis gives a finite-step, finite-history risk bound and sufficient conditions for user-code refinement to improve on history initialization. Across six tasks spanning personalized classification, prediction, and generation, LINEUP leads on all 12 metrics, each averaged over three independent runs (e.g., reducing LaMP-3 RMSE by 11.4% relative to the strongest baseline). It maintains advantages under limited history. These results show that rich personalization can be supported primarily by reusable, conditionally composed shared capacity, while independent user adaptation remains confined to a tiny correction state.

</details>


### [92] [Why Does Adaptive Batching Help LLM Pretraining? A Perspective from Unbounded Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02355)
*Arda Fazla,Antesh Upadhyay,Ege C. Kaya,M. Berk Sahin,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义BG-a噪声模型，建立依赖增长指数的下界Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})，并通过自适应批次大小调度器在OLMo2预训练中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型预训练中增大批次大小虽常见，但理论依据不足；现有随机优化分析假设梯度方差一致有界，但在非凸问题中常失效；BG-0模型允许方差随初始化距离二次增长，但过于保守；需要更精确刻画实际噪声行为的模型。

Method: 提出广义BG-a噪声模型（a∈[0,2]），在L-光滑假设下推导依赖增长指数的信息论下界Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})，并通过随迭代远离初始化点增加批次大小给出匹配上界；设计自适应批次调度器动态调整批次大小以控制方差增长。

Result: 在C4上预训练高达1B参数的OLMo2模型时，该调度器在相同token预算下比小批次和大批次训练获得更低验证损失，且迭代次数不足小批次训练的10%。

Conclusion: 广义BG-a模型更紧致地描述LLM预训练中的方差增长；自适应批次调度器能有效平衡方差控制与计算效率，为大规模预训练提供理论指导的实用策略。

Abstract: Increasing the batch size during training is a common practice in large language model (LLM) pretraining, yet the theoretical justification behind its success is not well understood. Analyses of stochastic optimization often assume uniformly bounded stochastic gradient variance, yet recent evidence suggests that this assumption fails in many practical nonconvex problems. The Blum--Gladyshev (BG-$0$) noise model relaxes this assumption by allowing the variance to grow quadratically with the distance from initialization, suggesting that batch size schedulers can help by controlling the variance growth during training. However, this growth can be overly conservative in practice. We empirically investigate variance growth in LLM pretraining and observe that a generalized BG model with a tunable growth exponent provides a tighter description of practical noise behavior. Motivated by this observation, we introduce the generalized BG-$a$ noise model, which interpolates between bounded variance ($a=0$) and BG-$0$ noise ($a=2$). Under $L$-smoothness, we derive an information-theoretic lower bound with growth-dependent oracle complexity $Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})$ and establish a matching upper bound in $ε$-dependence by increasing the batch size as the iterates move away from initialization. Finally, we propose an adaptive batch scheduler that controls variance growth through dynamic batch size adjustments during training. In pretraining OLMo2 models of up to 1B parameters on C4, our scheduler achieves a lower validation loss than both small and large batch training under matched token budgets, while using less than 10\% of the iterations of small batch training.

</details>


### [93] [Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02359)
*Doseok Jang,Jon Ander Campos,Youran Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation (LMOPD)，一种多教师蒸馏方法，通过显式优先级顺序整合专家策略，解决多奖励后训练中标量化或专家组合忽略优先级的问题，确保高优先级能力（如正确性）不被低优先级目标（如简洁性）牺牲。实验在 30B-A3B MoE 模型上显示，LMOPD 在保留最高优先级能力方面优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多奖励后训练方法通常通过标量化奖励或组合专家策略来整合目标，但未显式保护奖励优先级顺序。当权衡不对称时（例如简洁性不应以牺牲正确性为代价），这会导致高优先级能力退化。需要一种方法在专家整合中强制执行词典序优先级。

Method: LMOPD 为每个学生采样选择第一个检测到缺陷的目标对应的专家，然后将该专家的中心化 log-policy 修正投影到不违背更高优先级专家的子空间中，从而局部移除与高优先级目标冲突的分量。采用词典序路由和投影机制实现显式优先级保护。

Result: 在两专家设置下，LMOPD 完全保留准确性和推理质量增益，同时获得 46.9% 的简洁性增益。四专家设置下，保留约 90% 的准确性增益和推理正确性增益，远超次优基线的约 57%。消融实验显示词典序路由优于随机路由，投影进一步增强顶层优先级能力。

Conclusion: LMOPD 通过显式优先级机制有效整合多专家策略，在保持最高优先级能力（正确性、推理质量）的同时获取低优先级改进（简洁性），证明了显式优先级在专家整合中的价值，优于现有标量化或无优先级的基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) usually optimizes answer correctness, yet useful language-model behavior also requires high-quality reasoning and concise responses. Existing multi-reward post-training methods typically scalarize rewards or combine specialists without explicitly protecting a reward priority order. This is problematic when trade-offs are asymmetric: conciseness, for example, should not improve at the cost of correctness. We introduce Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation (LMOPD), a multi-teacher method for integrating reward-specialized policies under explicit priorities. For each student rollout, LMOPD selects the specialist for the first objective whose gate detects a deficiency, then locally projects its centered log-policy correction to remove components that oppose higher-priority specialists. We evaluate 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts transformer models in two- and four-expert settings on three math benchmarks, measuring retained specialist gains. With two experts, LMOPD's point estimates fully retain the accuracy and reasoning-quality gains while acquiring $46.9\%$ of the conciseness gain. With four experts, it retains $\approx90\%$ of both the accuracy gain and reasoning-correctness gain, compared to only $\approx57\%$ by the next best evaluated baseline. Matched four-expertablations show that lexicographic routing outperforms random routing and that projection further strengthens both top-priority capabilities. Across both scales, LMOPD preserves the highest-priority capabilities more effectively than the existing baselines we evaluate, demonstrating the value of explicit priorities for specialist integration.

</details>


### [94] [ArrivalBench: Agent-Generated Data Pipelines Are Correct Once and Wrong Under Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02363)
*Pranay Kothari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ArrivalBench 通过在对抗性交付调度（延迟、重复、乱序、重试）下重新执行管道并与批量重算对比，揭示了单次执行评分遗漏的大量静默错误，区分了错误表（静默失败）与崩溃（显性失败），发现幂等性风险比顺序风险更常见，且风险警告能降低静默失败但增加崩溃率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅对管道运行一次快照测试，无法捕获真实流式环境中因交付调度异常导致的静默数据错误，且无法区分错误结果与崩溃，使得监控系统无法有效识别错误表。

Method: 构建 40 个任务，实现 ArrivalBench：在对抗性调度下重放管道，将最终状态与完整日志的批量重计算结果比对；区分“错误表”（静默失败）与“崩溃”（显性失败）；测试 11 个模型生成的管道，评估修复循环与风险警告干预的效果。

Result: 单次执行评分认证 86%–100% 的管道，但重放发现其中 7.0%–79.2% 存在静默错误；修复循环未缩小差距；幂等性风险导致失败多于顺序风险；风险警告将某模型静默失败从 48.2% 降至 10.5%，但崩溃率从 9.0% 升至 37.0%，总失败率仅从 51.0% 降至 44.0%；11 个模型独立重跑，指标波动 ≤5.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 单次执行评分严重高估管道正确性；ArrivalBench 揭示静默错误普遍存在，且幂等性是主要隐患；区分错误表与崩溃对监控和干预设计至关重要；风险警告可将静默失败转为可观测崩溃，但需权衡总体失败率。

Abstract: Benchmarks for agent-generated data work grade a pipeline by running it once against a fixed snapshot. ArrivalBench instead re-executes the pipeline an agent leaves behind under adversarial but replayable delivery schedules (late, duplicated, out-of-order and retried records) and requires its final state to equal a batch recomputation of the complete log. Because the oracle recomputes rather than classifies, a wrong table and a crash are distinct verdicts: a crash is visible to monitoring a team already runs, and a wrong table is not. On 40 tasks we built, our reimplementation of single-execution grading certifies 86-100% of the pipelines eleven models produce; re-executing the same artifacts finds 7.0-79.2% of the certified ones silently wrong. The gap is not produced by the repair loop: within the same model and task, pipelines repaired against the snapshot test fail replay about as often as those that passed it first time. In every model, idempotency hazards fail more often than ordering hazards. Separating a wrong answer from a crash also changes how interventions read: a hazard warning cuts one model's silent failure from 48.2% to 10.5% while raising its crash rate from 9.0% to 37.0%, so all-in failure moves only from 51.0% to 44.0%. All eleven arms were independently re-run, and rates moved by at most 5.9 points.

</details>


### [95] [Co-design Gym: A Unified Benchmark for Embodiment-Policy Co-optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02366)
*Aviraj Newatia,Yordan Tsvetkov,Leonard Pleiss,Andrew Spielberg,Rika Antonova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Co-Design Gym基准套件，包含20个环境家族和85+个协同设计预设，覆盖机器人操作/运动、多机器人协作、软体动力学、电网、无线网络、F1赛车、多智能体仓储等领域，并系统评估了代表性协同设计算法，为智能体本体与策略联合优化研究提供统一基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多假设智能体本体固定，仅关注策略学习；但本体与策略相互制约，分别优化高度次优。需建立系统性基准以显式研究本体-策略联合优化（协同设计）问题。

Method: 构建Co-Design Gym基准套件：跨10大领域的20个环境家族，提供85+个协同设计预设；并对代表性协同设计算法进行系统实证评估，刻画当前技术水平。

Result: 发布了首个大规模、多领域的本体-策略联合优化基准，并给出了主流协同设计算法的系统性能表征，确立了可比较的研究基线。

Conclusion: Co-Design Gym为协同设计研究提供了统一、可复现、可累积进步的基础设施，推动该领域从零散探索走向系统化、标准化发展。

Abstract: Finding an optimal behaviour policy within a given environment is a widely studied problem in domains as diverse as games, robotics, energy infrastructure, communication networks, and multi-agent systems. Numerous benchmarks have been developed to support such research, but the vast majority assume that the agent's embodiment (design) is fixed, focusing instead on policy learning alone. Lifting this assumption gives rise to a broader class of problems in which optimizing embodiment and policy separately is highly suboptimal. An agent's embodiment strongly shapes which control policies can be discovered, while the optimal embodiment is in turn defined by the policies it admits. To help the research community study this class of problems explicitly and systematically, we introduce Co-Design Gym - a suite of benchmark environments for jointly optimizing embodiment and policy. Our environments span domains such as robotic manipulation and locomotion, multi-robot cooperation, deformable and soft dynamics, video games, electricity grids, wireless networks, F1 racing, multi-agent warehouses, and optimal control, offering 20 environment families (domains), with over 85 distinct co-design presets in total. We further contribute a systematic evaluation of representative co-design algorithms, characterizing the current state of the art. Together, these contributions lay the groundwork for cumulative, comparable progress in co-design.

</details>


### [96] [Latent-MOPD: Latent Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02381)
*Zhengyu Fang,Seoyeon Hong,Jie Yang,Muyang Li,Koyoshi Shindo,Brandon Joseph Lwowski,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Latent-MOPD 是首个用于大语言模型的表征级多教师在线策略蒸馏方法，它同时利用专家模型的输出分布和隐藏状态来训练学生模型，无需额外训练教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多教师在线策略蒸馏仅通过专家的输出分布传递知识，缺乏对模型内部表示的利用。研究旨在开发一种方法，能够同时整合多个专家的预测分布和用于计算这些预测的隐藏状态，从而更全面地融合专家能力。

Method: Latent-MOPD 通过以下机制协调多专家的表征监督：根据师生关系选择后期层作为监督目标，使用共享投影桥接不等宽的隐藏层维度，按领域分组进行更新。每个教师的监督信号逐渐从隐藏状态转移到 token 预测，两个通道使用相同的路由专家。

Result: 在同族设置下，Latent-MOPD 在数学、代码、逻辑的九个基准上均超越 token-only、representation-only 和均匀平均基线；参数量与教师相当时，学生在多数基准上超越单基准最佳教师。跨族更大规模教师下，也在所有基准上超越单通道基线。控制实验显示：全层表征蒸馏在领域纯净更新时稳定，但在领域交织更新时崩溃。

Conclusion: 单个学生模型可以通过同时利用多个专家的输出分布和内部表征，成功整合多个专家的专长能力，验证了表征级多教师蒸馏的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on the responses it generates. Existing LLM multi-teacher OPD transfers what specialists predict through their output distributions. We introduce Latent-MOPD, to our knowledge the first representation-level multi-teacher OPD method for LLMs. It integrates existing specialists through both their predictions and the hidden states used to compute them, without additional teacher training. To coordinate representation supervision from multiple specialists, we select late-layer targets according to the teacher-student relationship, bridge unequal hidden widths with a shared projection, and group updates by domain. Each teacher's supervision gradually shifts from hidden states to token predictions, with both channels using the same routed specialist. In our main same-family setting, Latent-MOPD outperforms the token-only, representation-only and uniform-averaging baselines on all nine benchmarks across math, code and logic. With the same parameter count as each teacher, the student also surpasses the per-benchmark best teacher on a majority of these benchmarks. With larger, separately developed cross-family teachers, Latent-MOPD outperforms both single-channel baselines on all benchmarks. A same-family all-layer representation-only control remains stable with domain-pure updates but collapses when teacher domains are interleaved within an update. Our results show that a single student can integrate capabilities from several specialists through both their output distributions and internal representations.

</details>


### [97] [The Surprising Effectiveness of Shared Memory in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02383)
*Giovanni Monea,Keshav Ramji,Yousef El-Kurdi,Luis A. Lastras,Yoav Artzi,Nathan Godey,Ramón Fernandez Astudillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出循环预测Transformer (LPT) 及其混合变体，通过共享首次循环的键值缓存实现内存共享，在150M-1B参数规模下，五次循环下验证困惑度降低1.12-1.82，上下文内存减少76-79%，建立了新的质量-内存边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer每次循环都写入独立键值缓存，导致内存随计算量增长。现有推理时缩减缓存技术会损失质量。作者旨在预训练阶段实现内存共享，同时不牺牲甚至提升模型质量。

Method: 设计Looped Prediction Transformer (LPT) 及混合变体：仅首次循环写入完整键值缓存（共享内存），后续循环读取共享缓存并仅维护短窗口本地缓存。在FineWeb-Edu上预训练150M-1B参数模型，对比标准Transformer及循环基线。

Result: 五次循环下，混合LPT较同规模标准Transformer验证困惑度降低1.12-1.82，上下文内存降低76-79%。共享内存与本地内存形成差异化表示，后期循环主要关注共享内存，共享内存还作为梯度高速公路回传至首次循环。

Conclusion: 内存共享不损质量反而提升质量，共享内存机制使循环Transformer在参数效率与内存效率上同时突破，为大模型推理提供新范式。

Abstract: Looped Transformers apply the same layers several times per token, adding compute to improve quality without more parameters. Each recursion, however, writes its own key-value cache, so memory still grows with compute. Inference-time techniques can shrink this cache at a cost in quality. We pretrain looped language models to share memory: only the first recursion writes a cache, and later recursions read it while keeping a short window of their own. Surprisingly, we find that sharing memory does not cost quality and instead improves it. At 150M-1B parameters, our Looped Prediction Transformer (LPT) and its hybrid variant set a new quality-memory frontier for looped models: with five recursions, the hybrid lowers validation perplexity on FineWeb-Edu by 1.12-1.82 relative to a same-size standard Transformer while using 76-79% less context memory. Through an extensive analysis, we investigate why memory sharing helps. Shared and local memory develop different representations, and later recursions attend mostly to the shared memory, which also acts as a gradient highway to the first recursion.

</details>


### [98] [Hesitation Has a Geometry: Entropy-Trained Hyperbolic Probes for Sparse Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02391)
*Zeyong Zhang,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Tengfei Ma,Mengjia Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲熵引导（HEST），将隐藏状态嵌入庞加莱球，仅在下一token熵超过阈值时沿测地线引导，显著提升数学推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型数学推理呈层级树状结构，适合双曲空间嵌入；现有激活引导在每个token添加固定欧氏向量，忽略多数token已由上下文确定，导致干扰。

Method: 轻量探针以模型自带下一token熵为标签，将隐藏状态嵌入庞加莱球；当熵超阈值，沿Busemann读出的理想点测地线最速下降方向移动嵌入状态，再映射回隐藏空间；证明固定步长在任意状态下降量相同。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math和Llama-3.1三模型上，HEST+Busemann读出在MATH-500和GSM8K六项中五项提升高达1.8分；对比每token加对比向量反而降分；欧氏探针同训练方式下增益消失；提升主要来自模型犹豫高的问题，其余问题准确率近乎不变。

Conclusion: 基于熵的双曲空间引导能精准干预模型不确定的推理分支点，避免对确定性token的过度干扰，为数学推理提供更有效的激活引导范式。

Abstract: When a large language model solves a mathematical problem, its reasoning is largely hierarchical, and the solution often branches at a few tokens where the next-token entropy is high. Such tree-like structure embeds in hyperbolic space with far lower distortion than in Euclidean space. Activation steering, however, usually edits the hidden states of a pretrained model by adding one fixed Euclidean vector at every token, even though most tokens of a solution are already determined by the context. We propose Hyperbolic Entropy Steering (HEST), which embeds the hidden states in the Poincaré ball with a lightweight probe whose only label is the model's own next-token entropy. Where this entropy exceeds a threshold, HEST moves the embedded state along the geodesic of steepest descent of a readout of the probe and maps the change back to the hidden state. For the Busemann readout of a learned ideal point, we prove that a step of fixed length lowers it by the same amount at every state. On three instruction-tuned models from the Qwen2.5-Math and Llama-3.1 families, HEST with the Busemann readout improves greedy accuracy on MATH-500 and GSM8K in five of six settings, by up to 1.8 points, whereas a contrastive steering vector added at every token lowers accuracy. With a Euclidean probe trained in the same way, this gain disappears on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct. The gains are largest on problems where the model hesitates often, and accuracy on the remaining problems is almost unchanged.

</details>


### [99] [Inherit-MAS: Test-Time Evolution of Multi-Agent Systems through Workflow and Execution Inheritance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02396)
*Songtao Wei,Yi Li,Zhichun Guo,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Inherit-MAS，一种受生物进化启发的多智能体系统，在工作流层面和执行层面显式引入继承机制。通过元模型合成带角色、通信输入、工具权限的工作流，配合独立提示的评判者打分与诊断，利用工作流继承从最新候选起步、剔除无用节点并应用验证编辑，以及执行继承在请求与上下文完全匹配时复用存储结果，避免冗余计算。在 WorkBench 与 HotpotQA FullWiki 基准上，使用 GPT-4o-mini 与 Qwen3-32B 工作者均超越 EvoAgent、EvoMAS、TacoMAS 等演化基线，且执行继承显著降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统难以预先设计有效工作流，测试时演化虽能利用执行反馈优化，但大幅修改易破坏有用组件，重复执行不变请求又造成冗余计算。受生物进化中遗传与选择相互作用的启发，需要在工作流与执行两个层面显式引入继承机制，实现精准优化与计算复用。

Method: 1. 元模型合成初始工作流，声明工作者角色、通信输入、工具权限。2. 独立提示的评判者对每个执行候选打分并诊断缺陷。3. 普通优化轮次：工作流继承从最新完成候选出发，丢弃被判定无用的可移除节点，应用经验证的编辑以解决诊断出的缺陷。4. 执行继承：仅当完整解析后的请求与执行上下文完全匹配时，才继承符合条件的存储结果，避免重复模型与工具调用。

Result: 使用 GPT-4o-mini 工作者时，WorkBench 完成率 55.4%，HotpotQA FullWiki 联合 F1 49.7%，均优于 EvoAgent、EvoMAS、TacoMAS。使用 Qwen3-32B 工作者时同样超越上述基线。对比禁用执行继承的同控制器重跑，执行继承使 WorkBench 与 HotpotQA 的工作者 token 用量分别降低 29.1% 与 34.6%，总 token 用量分别降低 5.3% 与 18.1%。

Conclusion: Inherit-MAS 通过显式的工作流继承与执行继承，有效解决了多智能体系统测试时演化中的破坏性修改与冗余计算问题，在多个基准上实现了性能提升与显著的计算资源节省，验证了受生物进化启发的显式继承机制在多智能体工作流优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built from large language models coordinate specialized agents to tackle complex tasks, but effective workflows are difficult to design in advance. Test-time evolution refines workflows using execution feedback, yet broad revisions can disturb useful components, while re-executing unchanged requests can incur redundant computation. Inspired by the interplay of inheritance and selection in biological evolution, we introduce Inherit-MAS, which makes inheritance explicit at the workflow and execution levels. A meta-model first synthesizes a workflow of worker agents with declared roles, communication inputs, and tool permissions, and a separately prompted judge scores each executed candidate and diagnoses its deficiencies. In ordinary refinement rounds, \emph{workflow inheritance} starts from the latest completed candidate, may discard removable nodes judged unhelpful, and applies a validated edit to address the diagnosed deficiency. When the new candidate executes, \emph{execution inheritance} inherits eligible stored results only if the complete resolved request and execution context match, avoiding redundant model and tool calls. With GPT-4o-mini workers, Inherit-MAS achieves 55.4\% completion on WorkBench and 49.7\% joint F1 on HotpotQA FullWiki, outperforming EvoAgent, EvoMAS, and TacoMAS. With Qwen3-32B workers, it also exceeds these evolving-MAS baselines on both benchmarks. Compared with rerunning the same controller with execution inheritance disabled, execution inheritance reduces worker-token usage by 29.1\% on WorkBench and 34.6\% on HotpotQA, and total token usage by 5.3\% and 18.1\%.

</details>


### [100] [Validated Data Onboarding for AI Demand Forecasting on U.S. Building Meter Data: Design, Controlled Evaluation, and a Corrected Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02397)
*Yixuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据导入流水线，可在模型训练前仅使用预测时刻可用的历史信息检测并修复电力计量数据中的缺陷（缺失读数、传感器冻结、零值读数、单位变更等），实验表明该流水线能将预测精度恢复至清洁数据水平，并在多种模型、缺陷率和预测时跨下均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力公用事业和电网运营商越来越依赖机器学习模型进行次日需求预测，但训练数据常存在常规缺陷：读数缺失、传感器冻结、建筑长时间读零、单位变更百倍等。这些缺陷会严重降低预测模型性能，亟需一种仅利用预测时刻可用信息的缺陷检测修复方法。

Method: 构建仅使用历史信息的数据导入流水线，在公开的 Building Data Genome 2 数据集（12 栋美国建筑，210,528 条小时级记录，2016-2017 年）上开展受控实验：人工植入哈希记录的缺陷（触及 0.10% 和校准后的 1.6% 训练行），对比梯度提升、岭回归、随机森林三种模型在 1-24 小时预测时跨下的平均绝对缩放误差（MASE），并引入逐建筑校准机制防止过度清洗。

Result: 植入 0.10% 缺陷使梯度提升模型误差上升 86%（MASE 从 0.760 升至 1.415），经检测修复后误差降至 0.729（优于清洁基线），保留 93% 训练目标；在 1.6% 缺陷率下，无保护模型误差达季节性朴素基准的 4.4 倍，修复后模型再次匹配清洁基线。该模式在岭回归、随机森林及 1-24 小时所有时跨均成立。初版流水线因过度清洗导致预测恶化 25%，经逐建筑校准修正。所有结果可从固定公开输入经 SHA-256 验证、84 项自动化测试及持续集成复现。

Conclusion: 该数据导入流水线能有效利用仅历史信息检测修复电力计量缺陷，使多种预测模型在不同缺陷率和预测时跨下均恢复至清洁数据性能水平。过度清洗问题已通过逐建筑校准解决，方法具有完全可复现性。

Abstract: Electric utilities and grid operators increasingly rely on machine-learning models to forecast next-day demand, and those models learn from meter data that is routinely defective: readings go missing, sensors freeze, buildings read zero for hours, and units change by a factor of 100. This report presents a data-onboarding pipeline that detects and repairs such defects before a model is trained, using only information available at forecast time, and a controlled experiment that measures whether the pipeline protects a 24-hour-ahead forecast. On hourly electricity data for twelve U.S. buildings from the public Building Data Genome 2 dataset (210,528 rows, 2016-2017), seeded, hash-logged defects touching 0.10% of the training period raised the error of a gradient-boosting forecaster by 86%; after detection and past-only repair the error returned to the clean-data level (mean absolute scaled error 0.760 clean, 1.415 corrupted, 0.729 repaired) while 93% of training targets were retained. At a defect prevalence calibrated to published field studies (1.6% of training rows) the unprotected forecaster's error reached 4.4 times that of a seasonal-naive rule, and the repaired forecaster again matched the clean baseline. The same pattern held for ridge regression and a random forest and across horizons of 1 to 24 hours. A first version of the pipeline over-cleaned natural data and made forecasts 25% worse; that result is retained, its cause is traced in the published artifacts, and the per-building calibration that corrects it is documented as a dated amendment. Every number is reproducible from pinned public inputs with SHA-256 verification, 84 automated tests and continuous integration.

</details>


### [101] [VisAudit: Evaluating Multimodal Agents for Visual Diagnosis and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02399)
*Shicheng Liu,Adam Kahirov,Qi Zhang,Zhimin Hu,Song Wang,Junhong Lin,Julian Shun,Yada Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VisAudit基准，用于评估多模态智能体在数据可视化任务中的自主审查能力，包括诊断、修复和验证三个维度。实验显示最强模型在自主修复轨道上仅能完全恢复47.4%的有缺陷图表，揭示了当前模型在自主审查方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体在数据可视化任务中虽有应用，但缺乏自主审查能力：无法像人类一样识别可视化错误、确定修改内容、在不破坏正确内容的前提下修复、以及验证干预是否成功。现有基准多评估预定义的单一能力（如图表生成、指令引导编辑、缺陷检测），无法捕捉自主审查的综合差距。

Method: 构建VisAudit基准，包含三个轨道：诊断修复、自主修复、开放世界验证。通过受控扰动验证过的源可视化生成1900个有缺陷实例（覆盖21种图表类型、10类缺陷）及300个初始正确图表。提供渲染图表及可配置辅助证据（源数据表、预期文本摘要、可视化代码），智能体迭代诊断缺陷、修改执行代码、检查执行与视觉反馈、判断是否需进一步干预。采用系统验证和人工对齐的质量控制确保注入缺陷定义明确且可从证据中恢复。

Result: 在领先多模态模型上实验显示，自主审查能力存在巨大差距：最强模型在自主修复轨道上仅完全恢复47.4%的有缺陷图表。

Conclusion: VisAudit填补了可视化自主审查评估的空白，实证表明当前最先进的多模态模型距离可靠的自主审查仍有显著差距，未来研究需重点提升智能体的诊断、修复与验证闭环能力。

Abstract: Multimodal agents are increasingly used for data visualization tasks but remain limited in autonomous review. Unlike humans, they may fail to recognize when a visualization is incorrect, determine what to change, repair it without disrupting correct content, and verify whether the intervention succeeded. Existing benchmarks largely evaluate predefined individual capabilities such as chart generation, instruction-guided editing, or defect detection, and therefore do not capture this gap in autonomous review. We introduce VisAudit, a benchmark for evaluating visualization diagnosis, repair, and verification. Given a rendered chart and configurable auxiliary evidence, including its source data table, intended text summary, and visualization code, an agent iteratively diagnoses potential defects, modifies and executes visualization code, inspects execution and visual feedback, and determines when no further intervention is needed. VisAudit defines three tracks spanning diagnosed repair, autonomous repair, and open-world verification, and contains 1,900 flawed instances across 21 chart types and 10 flaw categories, together with 300 initially correct charts. We construct the benchmark through controlled perturbations of validated source visualizations, with systematic verification and human-aligned quality control to ensure that injected defects are well-defined and recoverable from the available evidence. Experiments with leading multimodal models reveal a substantial gap from reliable autonomous review: the strongest evaluated model fully recovers only $47.4\%$ of flawed charts in the autonomous-repair setting.

</details>


### [102] [Trained Agentic Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02404)
*Bryce Sandlund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的工具增强方法，让小模型通过自我调用和上下文读取工具处理长文档，仅用8K上下文即可在OOLONG-synth基准上匹配GPT-5.4的1M上下文性能（文档长度>40K时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文模型通常需要原生训练长上下文或设计复杂的长上下文框架，计算成本高且工程复杂。作者希望探索一种极简的工具增强范式，使小模型能高效处理超长文档。

Method: 构建一个极简工具框架：包含两个工具——（1）自我调用工具：允许模型用任意指定提示词调用自身；（2）上下文读取工具：允许模型读取输入上下文中指定token范围的内容。在多样化合成数据集上微调Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型。

Result: 在OOLONG-synth基准测试中，当文档长度超过40K tokens时，仅拥有8K上下文窗口的微调模型性能与拥有1M上下文窗口的GPT-5.4持平。

Conclusion: 通过极简的工具增强（自我调用+上下文读取），小模型无需原生长上下文训练即可实现超长文档理解，显著降低了长上下文建模的计算和工程门槛。

Abstract: We study long context language models. Instead of training long context natively, or designing a long context harness, we train a model over the simplest possible harness: a tool to call itself with any specified prompt and a tool to read tokens in a range from the input context. We finetune Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on a diverse synthetic dataset using this harness. With only 8,000 tokens of context, our small model is as strong as GPT-5.4 with 1M tokens of context on the OOLONG-synth benchmark when document length exceeds 40K tokens.

</details>


### [103] [Efficient Neural Field Learning via Adaptive Coverage and Focused Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02410)
*Guang Zhao,Xihaier Luo,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Seungjun Lee,Shinjae Yoo,Yihui Ren,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACES（自适应覆盖感知高效采样），一种结构化采样框架，通过解耦覆盖性与重要性，利用自适应空间划分保证域覆盖并减少冗余，结合区域级重要性加权优先训练信息丰富区域，显著提升隐式神经表示（INR）在科学场学习任务中的收敛速度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 INR 训练常采用均匀子采样，忽略空间异质性导致效率低下；现有自适应采样虽优先高误差点，但多在点级操作，易造成局部冗余采样且域覆盖不足。需一种能同时保证全域覆盖、减少冗余并聚焦信息量大区域的采样策略。

Method: ACES 框架包含两大核心：1) 自适应空间划分：构建自适应空间分区以确保域覆盖并降低区域内冗余；2) 区域级重要性加权：对分区施加重要性权重，训练时优先采样高信息量区域。理论分析表明自适应划分通过提高区域内同质性降低梯度方差，受控偏差的区域级加权可优于标准无偏估计器。

Result: 在科学场学习任务实验中，ACES 比均匀采样和点级自适应采样基线收敛更快、误差更低，尤其在高度局部化复杂度的场中增益最大。

Conclusion: 通过解耦覆盖与重要性、引入结构化自适应分区与区域级加权，ACES 有效解决了 INR 训练中的采样冗余与覆盖不足问题，为高维连续场建模提供了更高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) provide a flexible framework for modeling high-dimensional continuous fields, but their training is often inefficient due to uniform subsampling that ignores spatial heterogeneity. Existing adaptive sampling methods partially address this issue by prioritizing high-error samples, but typically operate at the point level, often leading to redundant sampling in localized regions and insufficient coverage of the domain. We propose ACES (Adaptive Coverage-aware Efficient Sampling), a structured sampling framework that improves training efficiency by decoupling coverage and importance. ACES constructs adaptive spatial partitions to ensure domain coverage and reduce redundancy, and applies region-level importance weighting to prioritize informative regions during training. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that adaptive partitioning reduces gradient variance by increasing within-region homogeneity, and that controlled bias in region-level weighting may improve optimization efficiency relative to standard unbiased estimators. Experiments on scientific field learning tasks demonstrate that ACES achieves faster convergence and lower error than uniform and pointwise adaptive sampling baselines, with the largest gains in fields with highly localized complexity.

</details>


### [104] [Prompted to Discriminate: Generalizing Malicious-Input Probes in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02413)
*Elad David,Max Fomin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在LLM激活探针监控中添加分类后缀指令的效果，发现分类格式而非具体标准能显著提升分布外检测性能，且该收益可迁移到生产环境使用的多位置池化探针，但最佳后缀选择取决于模型和读出方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理越来越多地依赖激活探针作为运行时监控器来检测提示词注入、越狱和不安全请求。一种常见的低成本做法是在用户轮次后添加简短分类指令来锐化探针，但该后缀的措辞是否重要、其收益是否在探针未见过的攻击类型上依然成立（即部署监控器面临的实际场景）尚不明确。

Method: 采用严格的留一数据集外（LODO）评估，在13个安全基准（越狱、注入、良性聊天）和三个开放权重模型家族（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-12B）上，测试受控梯度的后用户后缀。对比了单位置探针和多位置池化探针（注意力、多最大值、MLP），并通过KV缓存分叉方式部署。

Result: 1) 分类后缀在单位置探针上持续优于无后缀（最多提升约4个AUC点）；2) 提示模型对输入进行分类（即使使用无内容标签）可靠获胜，离题或仅注意力后缀帮助很小；3) 收益来自分类格式而非命名标准：无内容后缀与真实恶意/良性后缀表现相当，标准仅在严格阈值下增加精确度；4) 该收益可迁移到生产环境使用的多位置池化探针，但最佳后缀取决于读出方式；5) 作为KV缓存分叉的廉价即插即用方案，但非自动获胜：哪个后缀有效及效果大小取决于模型和读出。

Conclusion: 分类后缀格式是提升激活探针分布外检测能力的关键因素，而非具体的分类标准。这一发现为部署低成本、高效的运行时安全监控提供了实用指导，但需根据具体模型架构和探针读出方式选择最优后缀。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on activation probes as runtime monitors for prompt injection, jailbreaks, and unsafe requests, reading the model's own hidden state to catch a harmful input before the agent acts on it. A cheap, increasingly common move, borrowed from LLM-as-judge prompting, is to append a short classification instruction after the user's turn and read the probe at that point, to sharpen it: the instruction asks the model to represent the incoming request as a class, concentrating the signal the probe must separate, at negligible serving cost. But does the wording of that suffix matter, and does its benefit hold in the wild, on attack types the probe never saw in training, the regime a deployed monitor faces? We test this with a controlled ladder of post-user suffixes under strict leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) evaluation across 13 safety benchmarks (jailbreak, injection, and benign chat) and three open-weight model families (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-12B). On a single-position probe, a classification suffix consistently improves out-of-distribution detection over no suffix (up to ~4 AUC points); yet which suffix matters: prompting the model to classify the input, even into content-free labels, reliably wins; an off-topic or merely-attentive suffix helps little. The gain comes from the classification format, not the named criterion: a content-free suffix matches the real malicious/benign one, with the criterion adding precision only at strict thresholds. This is not an artifact of the single-position read: the benefit carries to the multi-position pooling probes used in production (attention, multi-max, MLP), though the best-performing suffix there is readout-dependent. Served through a KV-cache fork, it is a cheap drop-in for any activation-probe monitor, though not an automatic win: which suffix helps, and by how much, depends on the model and the readout.

</details>


### [105] [The AI Theorist reveals excitonic structure in $α$-RuCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02417)
*Hongjian Zhou,Xianfan Nie,Sean Wu,Tarun Patel,Jinge Wu,Andrew Liu,Adam Wei Tsen,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Advances in experimental instrumentation and automation generate increasingly rich datasets, but turning experimental observations into microscopic understanding remains a bottleneck in scientific discovery. To accelerate this process, we introduce AI Theorist, a system of artificial intelligence (AI) agents for autonomous discovery of physical models through hypothesis generation, first-principles calculations and evidence-driven refinement. We apply the framework to $α$-RuCl$_3$, a leading candidate material for realizing a Kitaev quantum spin liquid, to investigate its electronic structure through optical spectra. AI Theorist develops a new interpretation of the optical and photocurrent observations, identifying distinct excitonic states with contrasting optical selection rules and real-space distributions. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an AI system autonomously developing a physical model to explain previously unpublished experimental observations in a quantum material, utilizing first-principles electronic-structure and many-body calculations. Our results establish a route to autonomous theoretical discovery in materials science, in which AI agents use first-principles calculations to turn experimental observations into physical models and testable predictions.

</details>


### [106] [Geometry-Aware Time Reparameterization for Flow-Map Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02427)
*Félix Dedek,Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知的时间重参数化方法，用于流映射蒸馏，通过分配更多学生时间给法向加速度大的轨迹段，提升单步和少步生成的样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流映射蒸馏在单步和少步生成中存在困难，假设法向加速度大的轨迹段更难蒸馏，因此需要改变教师的时间参数化以使有限时间过渡更易学习。

Method: 提出几何感知时间重参数化，推导共享时钟以均衡群体法向加速度统计量，构建实用近似，并将其融入拉格朗日流映射蒸馏中，使用变换后的时间坐标条件化学生网络。时钟在蒸馏前一次性估计，无需教师重训练或额外学生参数和推理时网络评估。

Result: 在合成数据、CIFAR-10和CelebA-64上实验表明，在匹配推理预算下，相比恒等时间蒸馏，样本质量显著提升，包括单步和两步图像生成的改进。

Conclusion: 时间重参数化在蒸馏过程中能有效改善单步生成性能，验证了几何感知时钟的有效性，为少步生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Flow-map distillation enables one- and few-step generation by learning finite-time transitions of a pretrained generative ODE. We investigate whether changing the teacher's time parameterization can make these transitions easier to learn. Motivated by the hypothesis that trajectory segments with large normal acceleration are harder to distill, we propose a geometry-aware time reparameterization that allocates more student time to these regions while preserving the teacher's geometric paths and terminal distribution. We derive a shared clock that equalizes a population normal-acceleration statistic under suitable assumptions, and construct a practical approximation from robust, regularized estimates across teacher trajectories. We incorporate this clock into Lagrangian flow-map distillation, using the transformed time coordinate to condition the student. The clock is estimated once before distillation and requires neither teacher retraining nor additional student parameters or inference-time network evaluations. Experiments on synthetic data, CIFAR-10, and CelebA-64 show improved sample quality over identity-time distillation at matched inference budgets, including improvements in one- and two-step image generation. The gains in one-step generation, where no intermediate sampling times can be adjusted, highlight the benefits of time reparameterization during distillation.

</details>


### [107] [CRISP: A Framework for Clause-Reconstructed Interpretable NeuroSymbolic Propositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02431)
*Alex Chan,Shafi Muhtasim Chowdhury,Ekin Can Erkuş,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Alex Yakovlev,Rishad Shafik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CRISP框架，通过将二值神经网络教师模型的最后一层激活向量（LLAV）重构为Tsetlin机器（TM）子句，实现对二值神经网络隐藏层决策证据的符号化可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽通过分层数值变换实现高准确率，但其决策过程难以审计，因为决策证据编码在隐藏激活中而非显式规则。现有方法缺乏从命名输入阈值到命名教师神经元的直接符号追踪路径，特别是针对二值神经网络的可解释性研究不足。

Method: CRISP框架包含三个核心步骤：1) 对教师模型的倒数第二层预logit激活进行符号二值化；2) 为每个LLAV神经元分配一个独立Tsetlin机器（ITM）；3) 将每个重构的隐藏位表示为布尔化输入特征上的命题子句，建立从输入阈值到教师神经元的直接符号映射。实验使用BinaryConnect CNN（BCCNN）和全二值神经网络（BNN）作为教师模型，在MNIST、KMNIST、FMNIST、SVHN、CIFAR10上评估，并研究二值阈值化、温度计编码、四分位分箱等多种布尔化策略。

Result: 主要发现：1) LLAV符号二值化在测试的BNN设置中不降低教师头准确率；2) ITM重构误差是主要限制因素；3) 四分位一位布尔化在SVHN上达到87.52%测试重构保真度，在CIFAR10上具竞争力；4) 重构LLAV在FMNIST上保留78.41%教师头准确率；5) 合并子句证据可视化显示学习的ITM字面量集中于中心基准数据集的目标区域。

Conclusion: CRISP提供了一条子句级路径，用于检查二值神经教师模型的最终隐藏表示，通过将隐藏激活转化为可解释的命题逻辑子句，实现了从输入特征阈值到输出神经元的透明追踪，为二值神经网络的可审计性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve high accuracy through layered numerical transformations, yet their decisions remain difficult to audit because decision evidence is encoded in hidden activations rather than explicit rules. This paper introduces CRISP, a framework that reconstructs the last-layer activation vector (LLAV) of binary neural teachers as Tsetlin Machine (TM) clauses. CRISP sign-binarizes the teacher's penultimate pre-logit activations, and assigns one Individual TM (ITM) to each LLAV neuron. Each reconstructed hidden bit is represented by propositional clauses over Booleanized input features, which gives a direct symbolic trace from named input thresholds to a named teacher neuron. CRISP is evaluated on MNIST, KMNIST, FashionMNIST (FMNIST), SVHN, and CIFAR10 using a BinaryConnect convolutional neural network (BCCNN) teacher and a fully binary neural network (BNN) teacher, with an additional study on binary thresholding, thermometer encoding, and quartile binning at multiple bit depths. The results show that LLAV sign-binarization does not reduce teacher-head accuracy in the tested BNN setting, while ITM reconstruction error is the main limiting factor. Quartile one-bit Booleanization gives the strongest reconstruction fidelity on SVHN at 87.52% test fidelity and is competitive on CIFAR10, and the reconstructed LLAV preserves 78.41% teacher-head accuracy on FMNIST. Pooled clause-evidence visualizations show that the learned ITM literals concentrate on the object region in centered benchmarks. CRISP therefore provides a clause-level route for inspecting the final hidden representation of binary neural teachers.

</details>


### [108] [Are you Synthesizing or Recalling? Evaluating LLMs on Algorithmic Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02438)
*Nickil Maveli,Antonio Vergari,Shay B. Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlgoREval 基准，系统评估大语言模型在已知算法代码生成中的参数化代码检索能力，发现模型在不同编程语言和输入表示上表现差异大，提示增强和微调能显著提升性能，强调需对 AI 生成的算法代码进行系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代码生成流程不透明，未显式区分算法知识回忆与推理应用。作者认为对于预训练语料中广泛存在的经典算法，代码生成本质是参数化代码检索（从内部知识复现命名算法），而非新颖合成，因此需要隔离评估这一能力。

Method: 构建 AlgoREval 基准，包含 599 道题目，覆盖 77 个经典算法、14 个领域、7 种编程语言、4 种图输入表示。在零样本设置下评估 15 个模型（7B–34B 参数），并实验对比提示增强（检索代码片段、结构化算法提示）、监督微调（SFT）和 GRPO 等方法的效果。

Result: 模型在不同语言和输入表示上的检索准确率差异显著，即使是文档丰富的算法也不例外。提示增强能提升复杂算法的准确率；SFT 带来更广泛的跨语言增益；GRPO 在特定语言上实现更大单语言增益。

Conclusion: 参数化代码检索是一个独特且可度量的能力。部署 AI 生成的算法代码前，必须进行系统性验证，不能盲目信任模型输出。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in code generation, where success depends on both recalling relevant algorithmic knowledge and reasoning about how to apply it. However, existing LLM pipelines are opaque, with no explicit separation between these two components. We argue that for well-known algorithms whose canonical implementations are widely accessible in pretraining corpora, code generation is better measured as \textit{parametric code retrieval}: reproducing a named algorithm from internalised knowledge rather than synthesizing a novel one. We introduce AlgoREval, a benchmark of 599 problems spanning classical 77 algorithms across 14 domains, 7 programming languages, and 4 graph-input representations to evaluate this capability in isolation, and assess 15 models (7B--34B parameters) in a zero-shot setting. We find substantial variation in retrieval accuracy across languages and input representations, even for widely documented algorithms and show that prompt augmentation with retrieved code snippets or structured algorithmic hints improve accuracy on complex algorithms, while SFT achieves broader language gains and GRPO achieves larger per-language gains on specific languages. Together, our results establish parametric code retrieval as a distinct, measurable capability and caution against deploying AI-generated algorithmic code without systematic validation.\footnote{Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Nickil21/AlgoREval

</details>


### [109] [A Generative Model of Complex Networks Using Graphons and Neural Inverse Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02439)
*Wooseong Choi,Italo'Ivo Lima Dias Pinto,Chen Sun,Gaurav Gupta,Dong Song,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种融合机制模型可解释性与深度生成模型摊销推断的生成图模型，引入多重分形分步图函数并通过神经逆算子实现参数恢复，在零样本图生成、单观测网络推断及EEG脑状态追踪任务中均表现优异。}


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成图模型存在互补优劣势：机制模型虽具可解释性但依赖实例特定的估计方法；深度生成模型虽提供摊销推断，但缺乏可解释性且受限于训练时见过的图规模。科学应用需要一个兼具两者优势的框架。

Method: 在函数空间中同时表述生成模型与参数恢复。提出多重分形分步图函数，通过递归构造扩展标准分步图函数，紧凑参数化复杂网络。该形式化允许训练神经逆算子以恢复参数，支持对未见图规模的推断。

Result: 仅在合成多重分形分步图函数实现上训练的模型，在零样本图生成基准测试中对四项指标中的三项取得最优平均性能，优于在实证网络上预训练的图基础模型；在单观测网络推断任务中表现与实例特定优化方法相当；在多受试EEG案例研究中，推断参数对可逆脑状态变化的追踪比传统网络统计量更敏感。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性与摊销推断可在生成图模型中有效统一，从而增强对复杂网络的理解。

Abstract: Generative graph models are central to understanding and simulating complex networks. However, existing approaches have complementary strengths and limitations. Mechanistic models offer interpretability but rely on instance-specific estimation methods. Deep generative models, on the other hand, offer amortized inference at the cost of interpretability and are largely limited to graph sizes seen during training. Scientific applications motivate a framework that retains the strengths of both paradigms. We bridge them by formulating both the generative model and parameter recovery in function space. A multifractal step graphon extends standard step graphons with a recursive construction that compactly parameterizes complex networks. This formulation admits a neural inverse operator to recover its parameters, enabling inference on unseen graph sizes. We evaluate our model, trained only on synthetic multifractal step graphon realizations, against both paradigms. Against a graph foundation model pretrained on empirical networks, our method achieves the best average performance on three of four metrics in a zero-shot graph-generation benchmark, indicating that the model transfers to real-world graphs. We also apply our method to single-observation networks, a regime largely inaccessible to deep models that require training corpora, where it performs comparably to an instance-specific method that optimizes on each graph. In a multi-subject EEG case study, the inferred parameters track a reversible change in brain state more sensitively than traditional network statistics. Together, these results indicate that mechanistic interpretability and amortized inference can be effectively unified in a generative graph model to enhance our understanding of complex networks.

</details>


### [110] [Bandits via Additive Quantized Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02440)
*Ami Tavory,Noam Touitou,Tal Sarig,Frank Cheng,Ido Guy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出残差量化（RQ）作为表示层，解决上下文老虎机中非线性奖励建模与在线效率的矛盾。RQ通过离线训练的码书将连续上下文映射为多层级离散质心分配，结合影子机制动态设定，实现了具有非线性表达能力且内存严格受限的加性老虎机算法。在13个数据集上，RQ变体在11个上优于非RQ基线，且以高达1000倍更少的内存匹配周期性重训练的XGBoost和神经网络基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文老虎机需要平衡非线性奖励建模与在线更新效率。树集成和神经网络虽能捕捉非线性，但需要周期性重训练和大量回放缓冲；线性模型虽支持O(1)内存的高效逐样本更新，但本质上受限于线性奖励结构。现有方法难以同时兼顾非线性表达能力、在线更新效率和内存约束。

Method: 提出残差量化（RQ）表示层：1）离线训练RQ码书，将连续上下文量化为多层级离散质心分配；2）引入影子机制动态设定量化层级；3）基于RQ表示构建加性老虎机算法，实现非线性表达能力与严格有界内存的结合。该方法避免了在线重训练和大规模回放缓冲的需求。

Result: 在13个数据集上进行实验：RQ变体在11个数据集上击败非RQ对应算法，优势往往显著；同时以高达1000倍更少的内存使用量，性能匹配采用倍增策略周期性重训练的XGBoost和神经网络基线。验证了RQ在非线性建模、在线效率和内存约束三方面的优势。

Conclusion: 残差量化作为轻量级表示层，成功弥合了非线性奖励建模与在线老虎机效率之间的鸿沟。RQ实现了严格有界内存下的非线性表达，无需在线重训练和大规模回放缓冲，为资源受限环境下的上下文老虎机提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Contextual bandits require balancing nonlinear reward modeling with online efficiency. Tree ensembles and neural methods capture nonlinearities but require periodic retraining and large replay buffers. Linear models update efficiently per observation with O(1) memory, but are fundamentally restricted to linear reward structures. We propose Residual Quantization (RQ) as a representation layer to bridge this gap. An offline-trained RQ codebook maps continuous contexts into discrete centroid assignments across multiple levels, set dynamically through a shadow mechanism. This enables a spectrum of additive bandit algorithms that achieve nonlinear expressivity with strictly bounded memory. Across 13 datasets, RQ variants beat their non-RQ counterparts on 11 of 13 datasets, often by wide margins, while matching doubling-retrain XGBoost and neural baselines using up to 1000 times less memory.

</details>


### [111] [AI-driven Thermal-aware Data Center Capacity Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02442)
*Yixing Li,Mark Fenton,Matthew Kaufeler,Ka Ming Leung,Xin Ai,Zhiyu Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AI驱动的热感知容量规划框架，通过毫秒级温度预测实现数据中心工作负载分布与HVAC冷却效率的秒级联合优化，较高保真CFD仿真加速10000倍且保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）训练推理产生的高密度GPU算力导致局部热点与突发热负载，传统高保真CFD仿真成本高昂且难以实时响应，现有AI模型要么预测误差大，要么假设过度简化，无法满足真实数据中心热感知规划需求。

Method: 构建嵌入式AI模型，学习机架功率、服务器功率、服务器布局、HVAC设置等关键参数，实现毫秒级温度场预测；基于此模型设计热感知容量规划框架，联合优化工作负载调度与HVAC控制策略。

Result: 在未见数据中心设计上测试，AI模型温度预测准确度高，推理速度比高保真CFD仿真快10000倍，框架可秒级输出最优热管理决策。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据中心设计者与运营商提供即时热感知决策能力，有效应对LLM时代算力密度激增带来的热管理挑战，实现冷却能效与算力部署的动态平衡。

Abstract: The emerging of large language models (LLMs) has posed significant challenges to the thermal management of data center. Intense GPU computation for LLMs results in localized hotspots. Moreover, spiking thermal loads during training and inference bursts make real-time cooling response more difficult to predict and control. Thermal-aware capacity planning of data center requires massive expensive high-fidelity CFD simulations. AI models can perform real-time prediction for unseen designs. However, existing works either have large prediction error, or have over-simplified assumptions for data center operations. This work presents an AI-driven framework that can perform thermal-aware capacity planning for a real-world data center in seconds. The embedded AI model learns from numerous key parameters (rack power, server power, server placement, HVAC settings etc.), and provides temperature prediction within milliseconds. This AI model is tested against high-fidelity CFD simulations, and results show that for unseen data center designs, model can achieve high accuracy with 10000X speedup. Driven by the AI model, the authors design the thermal-aware capacity planning framework. This framework can help data center designers and operators instantaneously optimize both workload distribution and HVAC cooling efficiency.

</details>


### [112] [A Composable AI-Accelerated Iterative Solver for 3D-IC Thermal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02461)
*Yixing Li,Jiahang Zhou,Zhiyu Zeng,Xin Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAIST 是一种基于域分解的 AI 加速迭代求解器，用于异构 2.5D/3D-IC 封装的热分析。它将全局仿真分解为块级子域问题，用神经算子替代子域求解器，通过迭代交换界面温度和热流耦合。该方法消除了整体模型的拓扑锁定问题，实现跨拓扑复用，并在两个先进封装系统上实现高达 178× 加速，平均温度误差分别为 0.068% 和 0.323%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 FEM 全封装热仿真计算成本极高（需数小时），而现有的 AI 代理模型将整个堆叠视为单一预测目标，一旦芯片数量或拓扑结构变化就必须重新训练，缺乏组合性和复用性。
